import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { SamplesResponseSchema } from '../src/core/index.js';
import { FIXED_NOW } from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
});

/** The samples as the server sends them: the order and the words the Menu must show. */
async function serverSamples(page: import('@playwright/test').Page) {
  const response = await page.request.get('/api/samples');
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
  return SamplesResponseSchema.parse(await response.json()).samples;
}

test.describe('menu: samples', () => {
  test('R1.1 lists the five samples in response order with blurb and dish names', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    const samples = await serverSamples(page);
    expect(samples).toHaveLength(5);
    await page.goto('/');
    const cards = page.getByRole('list', { name: 'Sample dinners' }).getByRole('listitem');
    await expect(cards).toHaveCount(5);
    await expect(cards.getByRole('heading', { level: 3 })).toHaveText(samples.map((s) => s.title));
    for (const [index, sample] of samples.entries()) {
      const card = cards.nth(index);
      await expect(card).toContainText(sample.blurb);
      await expect(card).toContainText(`Dishes: ${sample.dishes.join(', ')}`);
      await expect(card.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' })).toBeVisible();
    }
  });

  test('R1.3 "Plan this dinner" on the first sample opens Plan in one tap with the plan title', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    const [first] = await serverSamples(page);
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).first().click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/plan$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText(first?.plan.title ?? '');
  });

  test('R9.8 after the samples have loaded, choosing one and opening Plan makes no request', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' })).toHaveCount(5);
    const requests: string[] = [];
    page.on('request', (request) => requests.push(request.url()));
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).nth(1).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/plan$/);
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/plan$/);
    // Only the reload itself may fetch: its document, assets and (when on Menu) the samples.
    expect(requests.filter((url) => url.includes('/api/'))).toEqual([]);
  });

  test('R1.2 a failed samples request shows the failure copy and "Try again" lists them', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/samples', (route) => route.abort());
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(
      page.getByText('The sample dinners could not be loaded. Check your connection and try again.'),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' })).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.unroute('**/api/samples');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' })).toHaveCount(5);
  });

  test('R1.5 a second sample asks before replacing; Keep my dinner changes nothing', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    const samples = await serverSamples(page);
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).first().click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText(samples[0]?.plan.title ?? '');
    await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dinner' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Menu' }).click();

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).nth(1).click();
    const ask = page.getByText(`Replace your current dinner, ${samples[0]?.plan.title}?`);
    await expect(ask).toBeVisible();
    await expect(ask).toBeFocused();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Pick a dinner');

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep my dinner' }).click();
    await expect(ask).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).nth(1)).toBeFocused();
    await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dinner' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Plan' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText(samples[0]?.plan.title ?? '');
  });

  test('R1.5 Replace it loads the second sample', async ({ page }) => {
    const samples = await serverSamples(page);
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).first().click();
    await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dinner' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Menu' }).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).nth(1).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Replace it' }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/plan$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText(samples[1]?.plan.title ?? '');
  });
});

  // `loading` again, which starts the next fetch.
  let fetching = false;
  const fetchSamplesWhenWanted = (): void => {
    const state = store.getState();
    if (state.route === 'menu' && state.samples.status === 'idle') {
      store.dispatch({ type: 'samples/loading' });
      return;
    }
    if (state.samples.status !== 'loading' || fetching) return;
    fetching = true;
    void fetchSamples(env.fetch).then((result) => {
      fetching = false;
      store.dispatch(
        result.ok ? { type: 'samples/loaded', samples: result.value } : { type: 'samples/failed' },
      );
    });
  };

  const unsubscribe = store.subscribe(() => {
    apply();
    fetchSamplesWhenWanted();
  });
  apply();
  fetchSamplesWhenWanted();""")

cat > src/web/components/SampleList.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COPY, dishesLine } from '../copy.js';
import type { SampleItem } from '../state/api.js';
import { useAppState, useClock, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

/** The sample cards, or the loading or failed state (R1.1, R1.2). */
export function SampleList(): VNode {
  const samples = useAppState((state) => state.samples);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();

  if (samples.status === 'failed') {
    return (
      <div class="notice-block">
        <p>{COPY.samplesFailed}</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'samples/loading' })}
        >
          {COPY.tryAgain}
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (samples.status !== 'loaded') {
    return (
      <p aria-busy="true" class="muted">
        {COPY.samplesLoading}
      </p>
    );
  }

  const choose = (sample: SampleItem): void => {
    dispatch({
      type: 'dinner/load',
      nowMs: clock.nowMs(),
      load: {
        plan: sample.plan,
        source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: sample.id },
        kitchen: sample.kitchen,
        serve: null,
        suggested: sample.servingTime,
        route: 'plan',
        notice: null,
      },
    });
  };

  return (
    <ul class="card-list" aria-label={COPY.samplesHeading}>
      {samples.samples.map((sample) => (
        <li key={sample.id} class="card">
          <h3 id={`sample-${sample.id}-title`}>{sample.title}</h3>
          <p>{sample.blurb}</p>
          <p class="muted">{dishesLine(sample.dishes)}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button button--primary"
            data-sample-id={sample.id}
            aria-describedby={`sample-${sample.id}-title`}
            onClick={() => choose(sample)}
          >
            {COPY.planThisDinner}
          </button>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/screens/Menu.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { ConfirmInline } from '../components/ConfirmInline.js';
import { SampleList } from '../components/SampleList.js';
import { COPY, replaceAsk } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState, useClock, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

/** Choose a dinner: samples now; pasted recipes and a Dinnerfile follow in later tasks. */
export function Menu(): VNode {
  const pending = useAppState((state) => state.pendingLoad);
  const currentTitle = useAppState((state) => state.dinner?.plan.title ?? '');
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();

  const keep = (): void => {
    const sampleId = pending?.source.kind === 'sample' ? pending.source.sampleId : null;
    dispatch({ type: 'dinner/keep' });
    if (sampleId !== null) {
      // The question is gone after this render; put focus back on the card that was tapped.
      queueMicrotask(() => {
        document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-sample-id="${sampleId}"]`)?.focus();
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <main class="screen">
      <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{COPY.menuHeading}</h1>
      <p>{COPY.menuIntro}</p>
      <section aria-labelledby="samples-heading">
        <h2 id="samples-heading">{COPY.samplesHeading}</h2>
        {pending !== null && (
          <ConfirmInline
            text={replaceAsk(currentTitle)}
            confirmLabel={COPY.replaceIt}
            cancelLabel={COPY.keepMine}
            onConfirm={() => dispatch({ type: 'dinner/replace', nowMs: clock.nowMs() })}
            onCancel={keep}
          />
        )}
        <SampleList />
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
EOF

npx prettier --write src >/dev/null; sed -n 18,40p src/web/state/hooks.ts; npm run typecheck 2>&1 | grep error; (timeout 900 npx playwright test e2e/menu.spec.ts --project=phone 2>&1 | grep -E "✓|✘|passed|failed|Error:|Expected|Received" | head -20)
      const store = useContext(StoreContext);
      if (store === null) throw new Error('useAppState needs a StoreProvider');
      return store;
    }
    
    /** Reads a part of the state; re-renders when the selected value changes (`Object.is`). */
    export function useAppState<T>(select: (state: AppState) => T): T {
      const store = useStore();
      const [, rerender] = useReducer((n: number) => n + 1, 0);
      const value = select(store.getState());
      const latest = useRef(value);
      latest.current = value;
      useEffect(() => {
        const changed = (): boolean => !Object.is(select(store.getState()), latest.current);
        // The store may have changed between the render and this effect; do not miss it.
        if (changed()) rerender(0);
        return store.subscribe(() => {
          if (changed()) rerender(0);
        });
        // `select` is usually an inline function; the subscription only needs the store.
      }, [store]);
      return value;
    }

import { test } from '@playwright/test';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import { seedDinner } from './support.js';
test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London', viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 } });
for (const colorScheme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
  test(`shots ${colorScheme}`, async ({ page }) => {
    await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme });
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).first().waitFor();
    await page.screenshot({ path: `/tmp/md-shots/menu-${colorScheme}.png`, fullPage: true });
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).first().click();
    await page.screenshot({ path: `/tmp/md-shots/plan-${colorScheme}.png` });
    await page.goto('/#/menu');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' }).nth(1).click();
    await page.screenshot({ path: `/tmp/md-shots/replace-${colorScheme}.png` });
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start over' }).click();
    await page.screenshot({ path: `/tmp/md-shots/startover-${colorScheme}.png` });
    const w = await page.evaluate(() => [document.documentElement.scrollWidth, document.documentElement.clientWidth]);
    console.log(colorScheme, 'scroll/client', w);
  });
}
test('seeded steps', async ({ page }) => {

## What I looked at and what I changed
I took screenshots at 390×844 in light and dark of: the Menu (full page), Plan right after choosing a sample, the replace question, the "Start over" confirmation, and Steps with a seeded dinner. There was no horizontal page scroll (scrollWidth 390 = clientWidth). Buttons, the select and nav cells are at least 48 px tall with 8 px gaps, and the spacing, type scale and both themes match `ui.md`.

- **Fixed:** the flow nav wrapped into three ragged rows when the "unreachable" reasons were shown. It is now a 2×2 grid on phones and four columns at 40 rem and wider.
- **Fixed:** each nav link only covered its text, so tapping the edge of a cell did nothing. The link now fills the cell.
- **Bug found by the e2e tests and fixed:** `useAppState` missed a store change made between a render and its effect subscribing. With the fake clock, the samples could arrive in that gap and the Menu stayed on "Loading the sample dinners."

The scratch screenshot spec and the image files are deleted.

## Decisions I made
- **`navigate` carries `cookRegistered`:** the design lists `navigate {route}` but says `reachable` needs it, and `reduce` reads nothing outside its arguments.
- **`useClock()` in `hooks.ts`:** returns `systemClock`, so components can supply the `nowMs` that `dinner/load` and `dinner/replace` need.
- **`effects.ts` takes an env** (storage, window, document, fetch, `cookRegistered`) so it can be faked later. The samples are fetched only while the route is `menu`, once per page load, and again on "Try again".
- **"Keep my dinner"** puts focus back on the sample button that was tapped. "Cancel" on "Start over" puts focus back on "Start over".
- **A stored plan that has errors but parses** is kept, and the app opens it on Steps. Only a plan for which `validatePlan` returns `plan: null` starts the app fresh with the notice, as the design says.
- **`ErrorBoundary`** offers only "Reload" for now. The copy table also lists "Start over"; I left it out because a boundary that catches a render throw can't safely rely on the store. This is a small gap against R10.2, and T1.6 has no automated check for it.

## Not done or not verified
- No test checks that `theme.js` sets the theme before the first paint. The e2e tests only check `data-theme` after load and after a reload.
- `public/theme.js` and `persist.ts` each spell out the storage key `make-dinner:state` separately.

## Open question
The recorded Sunday roast plan is titled "Roast chicken with lemon and thyme", not "Sunday roast". So the Plan `<h1>` and the question "Replace your current dinner, <title>?" show the dish name. That is what the model returned, and I left the recordings untouched. Should the samples' plan title come from `samples/index.json` instead? That would change recipe-reader, not this spec.

    /** Reads a part of the state; re-renders when the selected value changes (`Object.is`). */
    export function useAppState<T>(select: (state: AppState) => T): T {
      const store = useStore();
      const [, rerender] = useReducer((n: number) => n + 1, 0);
      const value = select(store.getState());
      const latest = useRef(value);
      latest.current = value;
      useEffect(() => {
        const changed = (): boolean => !Object.is(select(store.getState()), latest.current);
        // The store may have changed between the render and this effect; do not miss it.
        if (changed()) rerender(0);
        return store.subscribe(() => {
          if (changed()) rerender(0);
        });
        // `select` is usually an inline function; the subscription only needs the store.
      }, [store]);
      return value;
    }
    
    export function useDispatch(): (action: Action) => void {
      const store = useStore();
      return store.dispatch;
    }
    
    /** The clock that components read when they dispatch an action carrying `nowMs`. */
    export function useClock(): Clock {
      return systemClock;
    }
    exit 0

    export function formatWeekday(ms: number, locale?: string): string {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { weekday: 'short' }).format(ms);
    }
    
    /** `null` for today, "tomorrow", "yesterday", else a short date like "Fri 13 Nov". */
    export function formatDay(ms: number, nowMs: number, locale?: string): string | null {
      if (sameLocalDate(ms, nowMs)) return null;
      const now = new Date(nowMs);
      const tomorrow = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1).getTime();
      const yesterday = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 1).getTime();
      if (sameLocalDate(ms, tomorrow)) return 'tomorrow';
      if (sameLocalDate(ms, yesterday)) return 'yesterday';
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
        weekday: 'short',
        day: 'numeric',
        month: 'short',
      })
        .format(ms)
        .replace(',', '');
    }
    
    /** "1 min", "45 min", "1 h", "1 h 50 min". */
    export function formatDuration(minutes: number): string {
      const whole = Math.max(0, Math.round(minutes));
      const hours = Math.floor(whole / 60);
      const rest = whole % 60;
      if (hours === 0) return `${rest} min`;
      return rest === 0 ? `${hours} h` : `${hours} h ${rest} min`;
    }
    exit 0

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && mkdir -p src/web/components test/web/components && cat > src/web/state/schedule-view.ts <<'EOF'
import type { HoldExceededIssue, Schedule, ScheduledStep, Step } from '../../core/index.js';

/** A schedule or a rebuilt schedule: the latter adds `servingMin` (rebuild design, "The axis"). */
export type AxisSchedule = Schedule & { servingMin?: number };

export interface ScheduleView {
  plan: Schedule['plan'];
  steps: readonly ScheduledStep[];
  warnings: readonly HoldExceededIssue[];
  /** 0 for a schedule output. */
  servingMin: number;
  /** The earliest start. */
  startMin: number;
  /** `servingMin - startMin`. */
  spanMin: number;
  stepById: ReadonlyMap<string, Step>;
  dishName: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
}

export function scheduleView(s: AxisSchedule): ScheduleView {
  return {
    plan: s.plan,
    steps: [],
    warnings: [],
    servingMin: 0,
    startMin: 0,
    spanMin: 0,
    stepById: new Map(),
    dishName: new Map(),
  };
}

export type StepMark = 'critical' | 'back-to-back' | null;

/** Critical with a hold is "critical"; critical that keeps is "back-to-back" (shown "No slack"). */
export function stepMark(s: ScheduledStep, step: Step): StepMark {
  void s;
  void step;
  return null;
}
EOF

cat > src/web/components/plan-list-items.ts <<'EOF'
import type { ScheduledStep } from '../../core/index.js';
import { compareIds } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY, laneName, listWait } from '../copy.js';
import {
  clockAt,
  formatClock,
  formatDuration,
  formatWeekday,
  sameLocalDate,
} from '../state/clock.js';
import { stepMark } from '../state/schedule-view.js';
import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';

export type ItemStatus =
  | { kind: 'critical' }
  | { kind: 'back-to-back' }
  | { kind: 'slack'; minutes: number };

export interface PlanListItem {
  stepId: string;
  range: string;
  title: string;
  detail: string | null;
  dishName: string;
  lanes: string;
  duration: string;
  status: ItemStatus;
  warning: string | null;
}

export interface PlanListGroup {
  minute: number;
  heading: string;
  items: PlanListItem[];
}

/** The lanes a step holds in text: appliance lane first, then the cook; "Hands-free" if none. */
function lanesOf(s: ScheduledStep): string {
  const names: string[] = [];
  if (s.lanes.burner !== null) names.push(laneName('burner', s.lanes.burner));
  if (s.lanes.oven !== null) names.push(laneName('oven', s.lanes.oven));
  if (s.lanes.cook !== null) names.push(laneName('cook', s.lanes.cook));
  return names.length === 0 ? COPY.handsFree : names.join(', ');
}

/**
 * Every step once, ordered by start then id, grouped by start minute (R7.1, R7.2). `chosenMs`
 * is minute 0 of the axis; the serving instant is `clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin)`.
 */
export function planListGroups(
  view: ScheduleView,
  chosenMs: number,
  locale?: string,
): PlanListGroup[] {
  const servingMs = clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin);
  const sorted = [...view.steps].sort((a, b) => a.startMin - b.startMin || compareIds(a.id, b.id));
  const waits = new Map(view.warnings.map((w) => [w.stepId, w]));
  const groups: PlanListGroup[] = [];

  for (const s of sorted) {
    const step = view.stepById.get(s.id);
    if (step === undefined) continue;
    const startMs = clockAt(chosenMs, s.startMin);
    const mark = stepMark(s, step);
    const status: ItemStatus =
      mark === 'critical'
        ? { kind: 'critical' }
        : mark === 'back-to-back'
          ? { kind: 'back-to-back' }
          : { kind: 'slack', minutes: s.slackMin };
    const warning = waits.get(s.id);
    const item: PlanListItem = {
      stepId: s.id,
      range: `${formatClock(startMs, locale)}–${formatClock(clockAt(chosenMs, s.endMin), locale)}`,
      title: step.title,
      detail: step.detail ?? null,
      dishName: view.dishName.get(step.dish) ?? step.dish,
      lanes: lanesOf(s),
      duration: formatDuration(step.minutes),
      status,
      warning: warning === undefined ? null : listWait(warning.waitMin, warning.holdMin),
    };
    const last = groups[groups.length - 1];
    if (last !== undefined && last.minute === s.startMin) {
      last.items.push(item);
    } else {
      const clock = formatClock(startMs, locale);
      const heading = sameLocalDate(startMs, servingMs)
        ? clock
        : `${formatWeekday(startMs, locale)} ${clock}`;
      groups.push({ minute: s.startMin, heading, items: [item] });
    }
  }
  return groups;
}
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/web/components/plan-list-items.pbt.ts <<'EOF'
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { schedule } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import { planListGroups } from '../../../src/web/components/plan-list-items.js';
import { scheduleView } from '../../../src/web/state/schedule-view.js';
import { arbPlanAndKitchen } from '../../support/arbitraries.js';
import { fcParams } from '../../support/fc-params.js';

const original = process.env['TZ'];
const CHOSEN_MS = Date.parse('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z');

describe('plan list properties', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    process.env['TZ'] = 'Europe/London';
    expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
  });
  afterAll(() => {
    if (original === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
    else process.env['TZ'] = original;
  });

  it('P-UI4 every step once, ordered by start then id, group minutes strictly increasing', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(arbPlanAndKitchen(), ({ plan, kitchen }) => {
        const result = schedule(plan, kitchen);
        expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
        if (!result.ok) return;
        const groups = planListGroups(scheduleView(result.value), CHOSEN_MS, 'en-GB');

        // Oracle: plain loops over the schedule's own start minutes.
        const startOf = new Map(result.value.steps.map((s) => [s.id, s.startMin]));
        const listed = groups.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => ({ id: i.stepId, minute: g.minute })));

        expect(listed.map((x) => x.id).sort()).toEqual(plan.steps.map((s) => s.id).sort());
        for (const item of listed) expect(item.minute).toBe(startOf.get(item.id));
        for (let i = 1; i < listed.length; i += 1) {
          const a = listed[i - 1];
          const b = listed[i];
          if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error('index');
          expect(a.minute < b.minute || (a.minute === b.minute && a.id < b.id)).toBe(true);
        }
        for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
          expect(groups[i - 1]?.minute).toBeLessThan(groups[i]?.minute ?? Number.NaN);
        }
      }),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });
});
EOF

        const state = withDinner();

/**
 * The load of a chosen sample (R1.3). The dinner's title is the sample's own title from
 * `samples/index.json`, not the title inside the recorded plan (decided with the user).
 */
export function sampleLoad(sample: SampleItem): DinnerLoad {
  return {
    plan: { ...sample.plan, title: sample.title },
    source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: sample.id },
    kitchen: sample.kitchen,
    serve: null,
    suggested: sample.servingTime,
    route: 'plan',
    notice: null,
  };
}""")

      return state;
    default:
      return reduceCook(state, action);""")

/** The button names of a stepper: "Fewer burners", "More burners". */
export function fewer(what: string): string {
  return `Fewer ${what.toLowerCase()}`;
}
export function more(what: string): string {
  return `More ${what.toLowerCase()}`;
}

export function startAt(time: string): string {
  return `Start at ${time}`;
}

/** "Serve at 19:30", with the day when it is not today: "Serve at 00:30 tomorrow". */
export function serveAt(time: string, day: string | null): string {
  return day === null ? `Serve at ${time}` : `Serve at ${time} ${day}`;
}

export function takes(duration: string): string {
  return `Takes ${duration}`;
}

/**
 * A hold warning (R5.6). `userTitle` is the title of the step that uses the result first;
 * null for a terminal step, whose result waits for serving.
 */
export function holdWarning(
  dish: string,
  title: string,
  waitMin: number,
  holdMin: number,
  userTitle: string | null,
): string {
  const until = userTitle === null ? 'serving' : `\\u201c${userTitle}\\u201d starts`;
  return `${dish}: \\u201c${title}\\u201d will be ready ${formatDuration(waitMin)} before ${until} and can wait only ${formatDuration(holdMin)}. More ovens, burners or cooks may help.`;
}

/** The missing appliance sentence: "This plan needs an oven and the kitchen has none. These steps use it: ...". */
export function missingAppliance(appliance: 'burner' | 'oven', titles: readonly string[]): string {
  const what = appliance === 'oven' ? 'an oven' : 'a burner';
  return `This plan needs ${what} and the kitchen has none. These steps use it: ${titles.join(', ')}.`;
}

cat > src/web/components/Stepper.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { fewer, more } from '../copy.js';

interface Props {
  /** The visible label and the group name: "Burners". */
  label: string;
  value: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
}

/** A count with "−" and "+" buttons, disabled at the bounds (R5.1). */
export function Stepper(props: Props): VNode {
  const id = `stepper-${props.label.toLowerCase()}`;
  return (
    <div class="stepper" role="group" aria-labelledby={id}>
      <span id={id} class="stepper__label">
        {props.label}
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="button stepper__button"
        aria-label={fewer(props.label)}
        disabled={props.value <= props.min}
        onClick={() => props.onChange(props.value - 1)}
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true">−</span>
      </button>
      <output class="stepper__value">{props.value}</output>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="button stepper__button"
        aria-label={more(props.label)}
        disabled={props.value >= props.max}
        onClick={() => props.onChange(props.value + 1)}
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true">+</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/KitchenControls.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { LIMITS } from '../../core/index.js';
import type { Kitchen } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { Stepper } from './Stepper.js';

/** Burners (0 to 8), ovens (0 to 4) and cooks (1 to 6) (R5.1). */
export function KitchenControls(): VNode | null {
  const kitchen = useAppState((state) => state.dinner?.kitchen ?? null);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  if (kitchen === null) return null;
  const set = (patch: Partial<Kitchen>): void =>
    dispatch({ type: 'kitchen/set', kitchen: { ...kitchen, ...patch } });
  return (
    <div class="kitchen-controls">
      <Stepper
        label={COPY.burners}
        value={kitchen.burners}
        min={0}
        max={LIMITS.burnersMax}
        onChange={(burners) => set({ burners })}
      />
      <Stepper
        label={COPY.ovens}
        value={kitchen.ovens}
        min={0}
        max={LIMITS.ovensMax}
        onChange={(ovens) => set({ ovens })}
      />
      <Stepper
        label={COPY.cooks}
        value={kitchen.cooks}
        min={LIMITS.cooksMin}
        max={LIMITS.cooksMax}
        onChange={(cooks) => set({ cooks })}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/ServingTime.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { ServeTimeSchema } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { formatDay } from '../state/clock.js';
import { useAppState, useClock, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

/** The serving time input (R5.1, R5.3); the day shows beside it when it is not today. */
export function ServingTime(props: { nowMs: number }): VNode | null {
  const serving = useAppState((state) => state.dinner?.serving ?? null);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();
  if (serving === null) return null;
  const day = formatDay(serving.atMs, props.nowMs);
  return (
    <div class="serving-time">
      <label for="serving-time">{COPY.servingTime}</label>
      <input
        id="serving-time"
        class="input input--mono"
        type="time"
        value={serving.time}
        onChange={(event) => {
          const parsed = ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(event.currentTarget.value);
          if (parsed.success) {
            dispatch({ type: 'serving/set-time', time: parsed.data, nowMs: clock.nowMs() });
          }
        }}
      />
      {day !== null && <span class="muted">{day}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { serveAt, startAt, takes } from '../copy.js';
import { clockAt, formatClock, formatDay, formatDuration } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';

/** Start time, serving time (with the day when not today) and span (R5.4). */
export function PlanSummary(props: {
  view: ScheduleView;
  chosenMs: number;
  nowMs: number;
}): VNode {
  const { view, chosenMs, nowMs } = props;
  const servingMs = clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin);
  return (
    <div class="summary">
      <p class="summary__start">{startAt(formatClock(clockAt(chosenMs, view.startMin)))}</p>
      <p>{serveAt(formatClock(servingMs), formatDay(servingMs, nowMs))}</p>

cat > src/web/components/WarningList.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { compareIds } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY, holdWarning } from '../copy.js';
import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';

/** The title of the step that uses `stepId`'s result first, or null for a terminal step. */
function firstUserTitle(view: ScheduleView, stepId: string): string | null {
  const start = new Map(view.steps.map((s) => [s.id, s.startMin]));
  const users = view.plan.steps
    .filter((s) => s.after.includes(stepId))
    .sort(
      (a, b) => (start.get(a.id) ?? 0) - (start.get(b.id) ?? 0) || compareIds(a.id, b.id),
    );
  return users[0]?.title ?? null;
}

/** One sentence per hold_exceeded warning, or the all-clear (R5.6). */
export function WarningList(props: { view: ScheduleView }): VNode {
  const { view } = props;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="warnings-heading">
      <h2 id="warnings-heading">{COPY.warningsHeading}</h2>
      {view.warnings.length === 0 ? (
        <p>{COPY.noWarnings}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul class="warning-list">
          {view.warnings.map((w) => {
            const step = view.stepById.get(w.stepId);
            if (step === undefined) return null;
            return (
              <li key={w.stepId}>
                <span class="mark mark--warn">! Warning</span>{' '}
                <span>
                  {holdWarning(
                    view.dishName.get(step.dish) ?? step.dish,
                    step.title,
                    w.waitMin,
                    w.holdMin,
                    firstUserTitle(view, w.stepId),
                  )}
                </span>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/MissingAppliance.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import type { Issue } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY, missingAppliance } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

/** Per missing appliance: which steps need it, and the way out (R5.7). */
export function MissingAppliance(props: { issues: readonly Issue[] }): VNode | null {
  const dinner = useAppState((state) => state.dinner);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  if (dinner === null) return null;
  const { plan, kitchen } = dinner;

  const needing = (appliance: 'burner' | 'oven'): string[] => {
    const ids = new Set<string>();
    for (const issue of props.issues) {
      if (issue.code === 'missing_appliance' && issue.appliance === appliance) ids.add(issue.stepId);
    }
    return plan.steps.filter((s) => ids.has(s.id)).map((s) => s.title);
  };
  const oven = needing('oven');
  const burner = needing('burner');

  return (
    <section class="missing-appliance" aria-labelledby="missing-heading">
      <h2 id="missing-heading" class="visually-hidden">
        {COPY.planHeading}
      </h2>
      {oven.length > 0 && (
        <div class="notice-block">
          <p>{missingAppliance('oven', oven)}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button button--primary"
            onClick={() =>
              dispatch({ type: 'kitchen/set', kitchen: { ...kitchen, ovens: 1 } })
            }
          >
            {COPY.addOven}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {burner.length > 0 && (
        <div class="notice-block">
          <p>{missingAppliance('burner', burner)}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button button--primary"
            onClick={() =>
              dispatch({ type: 'kitchen/set', kitchen: { ...kitchen, burners: 1 } })
            }
          >
            {COPY.addBurner}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div class="actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: 'steps', cookRegistered: false })}
        >
          {COPY.changeSteps}
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/PlanList.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COPY, slackLabel } from '../copy.js';
import { clockAt, formatClock } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';
import { planListGroups } from './plan-list-items.js';
import type { ItemStatus } from './plan-list-items.js';

function statusText(status: ItemStatus): string {
  if (status.kind === 'critical') return COPY.listCritical;
  if (status.kind === 'back-to-back') return COPY.listNoSlack;
  return slackLabel(status.minutes);
}

/**
 * The plan as text: always in the DOM, the accessible alternative to the timeline and the
 * print layout (R7.1 to R7.3). Items are focusable by script.
 */
export function PlanList(props: { view: ScheduleView; chosenMs: number }): VNode {
  const { view, chosenMs } = props;
  const groups = planListGroups(view, chosenMs);
  const servingMs = clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin);
  return (
    <section id="plan-list" aria-labelledby="plan-list-heading">
      <h2 id="plan-list-heading">{COPY.planListHeading}</h2>
      {groups.map((group) => (
        <div key={group.minute} class="plan-group">
          <h3 class="mono">{group.heading}</h3>
          <ol class="plan-items">
            {group.items.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.stepId} id={`step-${item.stepId}`} tabIndex={-1} class="plan-item">
                <p class="plan-item__title">
                  <span class="mono">{item.range}</span> {item.title}
                </p>
                <p class="plan-item__meta">
                  {item.dishName} · <span class="mono">{item.lanes}</span> · {item.duration} ·{' '}
                  <span class={item.status.kind === 'critical' ? 'mark mark--critical' : 'muted'}>
                    {statusText(item.status)}
                  </span>
                </p>
                {item.detail !== null && <p class="plan-item__detail">{item.detail}</p>}
                {item.warning !== null && <p class="mark mark--warn">{item.warning}</p>}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </div>
      ))}
      <div class="plan-group">
        <h3 class="mono">{formatClock(servingMs)}</h3>
        <ol class="plan-items">
          <li class="plan-item">
            <p class="plan-item__title">{COPY.listServe}</p>
          </li>
        </ol>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

cat > src/web/screens/Plan.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import { KitchenControls } from '../components/KitchenControls.js';
import { MissingAppliance } from '../components/MissingAppliance.js';
import { PlanList } from '../components/PlanList.js';
import { PlanSummary } from '../components/PlanSummary.js';
import { ServingTime } from '../components/ServingTime.js';
import { WarningList } from '../components/WarningList.js';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState, useClock, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { hashOf } from '../state/route.js';
import { scheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';
import { selectSchedule } from '../state/store.js';

/** Kitchen and serving time, then the schedule: summary, warnings, list (R5, R7). */
export function Plan(): VNode {
  const title = useAppState((state) => state.dinner?.plan.title ?? COPY.planHeading);
  const serving = useAppState((state) => state.dinner?.serving ?? null);
  const hasDinner = useAppState((state) => state.dinner !== null);
  const result = useAppState(selectSchedule);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();
  const nowMs = clock.nowMs();

  // Opened through the flow links with no serving time chosen yet: choose one (R4.11).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasDinner && serving === null) dispatch({ type: 'plan/enter', nowMs: clock.nowMs() });
  }, [hasDinner, serving]);

  return (
    <main class="screen">
      <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{title}</h1>
      <section aria-labelledby="kitchen-heading" class="no-print">
        <h2 id="kitchen-heading">{COPY.kitchenHeading}</h2>
        <KitchenControls />
        <ServingTime nowMs={nowMs} />
      </section>
      {result !== null && result.ok && serving !== null && (
        <PlanBody view={scheduleView(result.value)} chosenMs={serving.atMs} nowMs={nowMs} />
      )}
      {result !== null && !result.ok && result.error.kind === 'missing_appliance' && (
        <MissingAppliance issues={result.error.issues} />
      )}
      {result !== null &&
        !result.ok &&
        result.error.kind !== 'missing_appliance' && (
          <div class="notice-block">
            <p>{COPY.invalidPlan}</p>
            <a class="button" href={hashOf('steps')}>
              {COPY.goToSteps}
            </a>
          </div>
        )}
    </main>
  );
}

function PlanBody(props: {
  view: ReturnType<typeof scheduleView>;
  chosenMs: number;
  nowMs: number;
}): VNode {
  return (
    <>
      <PlanSummary view={props.view} chosenMs={props.chosenMs} nowMs={props.nowMs} />
      <WarningList view={props.view} />
      <PlanList view={props.view} chosenMs={props.chosenMs} />
    </>
  );
}

import { test } from '@playwright/test';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import { FIXED_NOW, seedDinner } from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London', viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, hasTouch: true });
for (const scheme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
  test(`shot ${scheme}`, async ({ page }) => {
    await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: scheme });
    await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 }).waitFor();
    await page.screenshot({ path: `/tmp/plan-${scheme}-top.png` });
    await page.screenshot({ path: `/tmp/plan-${scheme}-full.png`, fullPage: true });
    const m = await page.evaluate(() => ({
      sw: document.documentElement.scrollWidth, cw: document.documentElement.clientWidth,
      small: [...document.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,a')].map((e) => { const r = e.getBoundingClientRect(); return [e.textContent?.trim().slice(0,20) || e.getAttribute('aria-label'), Math.round(r.width), Math.round(r.height)]; }).filter(([, w, h]) => (w as number) < 48 || (h as number) < 48),
    }));

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/web/state/hooks.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("import { useContext, useEffect, useReducer, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';","import { useContext, useEffect, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';")
s+='''
/** The clock's time in ms, read again every `everyMs`; the component re-renders when it does. */
export function useNow(clock: Clock, everyMs: number): number {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => clock.nowMs());
  useEffect(() => {
    setNow(clock.nowMs());
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(clock.nowMs()), everyMs);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [clock, everyMs]);
  return now;
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)

  return `Serve at ${time}`;

import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';

export const PX_PER_MIN = 6; // a step of 8 minutes is a 48 px target (decided here)
export const LABEL_MIN_PX = 56; // shorter bars carry no label

export type RowKind = 'cook' | 'burner' | 'oven' | 'hands-free';
export type BarFill = 'oven' | 'burner' | 'plain';

export interface TimelineBar {
  stepId: string;
  title: string;
  leftPx: number;
  widthPx: number;
  fill: BarFill;
  dashed: boolean;
  showLabel: boolean;
  /** The mark ▲ is drawn only for `critical`. */
  critical: boolean;
}

export interface TimelineRow {
  key: string;
  kind: RowKind;
  lane: number;
  label: string;
  bars: TimelineBar[];
}

export interface TimelineTick {
  minute: number;
  leftPx: number;
  label: string;
}

export interface TimelineLayout {
  originMin: number;
  endMin: number;
  widthPx: number;
  rows: TimelineRow[];
  ticks: TimelineTick[];
  serveLeftPx: number;
  serveLabel: string;
}

export function layoutTimeline(
  view: ScheduleView,
  chosenMs: number,
  locale?: string,
): TimelineLayout {
  void view;
  void chosenMs;
  void locale;
  return {
    originMin: 0,
    endMin: 0,
    widthPx: 0,
    rows: [],
    ticks: [],
    serveLeftPx: 0,
    serveLabel: '',
  };
}

cat > test/web/components/timeline-layout.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { canonicalPlan } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import type { Plan, Schedule, ScheduledStep, Step } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import { layoutTimeline } from '../../../src/web/components/timeline-layout.js';
import type { TimelineBar } from '../../../src/web/components/timeline-layout.js';
import { scheduleView } from '../../../src/web/state/schedule-view.js';
import { H1_SCHEDULE, HOME_KITCHEN } from '../../support/fixtures.js';

const original = process.env['TZ'];
const at = (iso: string): number => Date.parse(iso);

beforeAll(() => {
  process.env['TZ'] = 'Europe/London';
  expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
});
afterAll(() => {
  if (original === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
  else process.env['TZ'] = original;
});

const WED_1930 = at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z');
const THU_0030 = at('2026-11-12T00:30:00Z');

/** A bar as the design's H1 table writes it: id left/width fill dashed label critical. */
function bar(b: TimelineBar): string {
  return [
    b.stepId,
    `${b.leftPx}/${b.widthPx}`,
    b.fill,
    b.dashed ? 'dashed' : '-',
    b.showLabel ? 'label' : '-',
    b.critical ? '▲' : '-',
  ].join(' ');
}

describe('layoutTimeline: the design H1 table at Wed 19:30 (R6.1 to R6.6)', () => {
  const layout = (): ReturnType<typeof layoutTimeline> =>
    layoutTimeline(scheduleView(H1_SCHEDULE), WED_1930, 'en-GB');

  it('R6.3 origin is 17:40 rounded down to 17:30 (minute -120), the axis ends at 0, 720 px wide', () => {
    const l = layout();
    expect([l.originMin, l.endMin, l.widthPx]).toEqual([-120, 0, 720]);
  });
  it('R6.4 nine quarter-hour ticks from 17:30 to 19:30 at 0, 90, … 720 px; the serve line at 720', () => {
    const l = layout();
    expect(l.ticks.map((t) => [t.label, t.leftPx])).toEqual([
      ['17:30', 0],
      ['17:45', 90],
      ['18:00', 180],
      ['18:15', 270],
      ['18:30', 360],
      ['18:45', 450],
      ['19:00', 540],
      ['19:15', 630],
      ['19:30', 720],
    ]);
    expect([l.serveLeftPx, l.serveLabel]).toEqual([720, 'Serve 19:30']);
  });
  it('R6.1 rows run Cook 1, Burner 1, Burner 2, Oven 1, Hands-free', () => {
    expect(layout().rows.map((r) => [r.kind, r.label])).toEqual([
      ['cook', 'Cook 1'],
      ['burner', 'Burner 1'],
      ['burner', 'Burner 2'],
      ['oven', 'Oven 1'],
      ['hands-free', 'Hands-free'],
    ]);
  });
  it('R6.1 R6.5 R6.6 every bar of every row, by the formula (minute - origin) x 6', () => {
    const rows = layout().rows;
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.bars.map(bar))).toEqual([
      [
        'chicken.prep 60/60 plain - label -',
        'greens.shred 120/30 plain - - -',
        'gravy.roux 600/12 burner - - ▲',
        'gravy.strain 672/12 plain - - ▲',
        'greens.toss 684/6 plain - - ▲',
        'chicken.carve 690/30 plain - - ▲',
      ],
      ['gravy.roux 600/12 burner - - ▲', 'gravy.simmer 612/60 burner dashed label ▲'],
      ['greens.boil 660/24 burner dashed - ▲'],
      ['chicken.roast 120/450 oven dashed label ▲'],
      ['chicken.rest 570/120 plain dashed label ▲'],
    ]);
  });
  it('R6.6 chicken.prep keeps and has slack 0: no critical mark; the bar still carries its title', () => {
    const prep = layout().rows[0]?.bars[0];
    expect(prep?.critical).toBe(false);
    expect(prep?.title).toBe('Season and truss the chicken');
  });
});

describe('layoutTimeline across midnight (R6.4)', () => {
  it('R6.4 serving Thu 00:30 labels the midnight tick with the weekday', () => {
    const l = layoutTimeline(scheduleView(H1_SCHEDULE), THU_0030, 'en-GB');
    expect(l.ticks.map((t) => t.label)).toEqual([
      '22:30',
      '22:45',
      '23:00',
      '23:15',
      '23:30',
      '23:45',
      'Thu 00:00',
      '00:15',
      '00:30',
    ]);
    expect(l.serveLabel).toBe('Serve 00:30');
  });
});

/** Builds a hand-written schedule: every step on the cook unless `free`. */
function handWritten(
  rows: readonly { id: string; start: number; minutes: number; free?: boolean }[],
): Schedule {
  const steps: Step[] = rows.map((r) => ({
    id: `x.${r.id}`,
    dish: 'x',
    title: `Step ${r.id}`,
    minutes: r.minutes,
    appliance: 'none',
    handsOn: r.free !== true,
    after: [],
    holdMin: 30,
  }));
  const plan: Plan = canonicalPlan({ title: 'Hand written', dishes: [{ id: 'x', name: 'X' }], steps });
  const scheduled: ScheduledStep[] = rows.map((r) => ({
    id: `x.${r.id}`,
    startMin: r.start,
    endMin: r.start + r.minutes,
    lanes: { burner: null, oven: null, cook: r.free === true ? null : 1 },
    slackMin: 5,
    critical: false,
    waitMin: 0,
  }));
  return {
    plan,
    kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN,
    steps: scheduled,
    spanMin: 60,
    chainMin: 60,
    warnings: [],
  };
}

describe('layoutTimeline: hands-free rows (R6.2)', () => {
  it('R6.2 two overlapping hands-free steps give Hands-free 1 and Hands-free 2', () => {
    const l = layoutTimeline(
      scheduleView(
        handWritten([
          { id: 'a', start: -30, minutes: 30, free: true },
          { id: 'b', start: -20, minutes: 20, free: true },
        ]),
      ),
      WED_1930,
      'en-GB',
    );
    expect(l.rows.map((r) => [r.label, r.bars.map((b) => b.stepId)])).toEqual([
      ['Hands-free 1', ['x.a']],
      ['Hands-free 2', ['x.b']],
    ]);
  });
  it('R6.2 steps that touch end to end share one row, labelled Hands-free', () => {
    const l = layoutTimeline(
      scheduleView(
        handWritten([
          { id: 'a', start: -30, minutes: 10, free: true },
          { id: 'b', start: -20, minutes: 20, free: true },
        ]),
      ),
      WED_1930,
      'en-GB',
    );
    expect(l.rows.map((r) => [r.label, r.bars.map((b) => b.stepId)])).toEqual([
      ['Hands-free', ['x.a', 'x.b']],
    ]);
  });
});

describe('layoutTimeline: bar width and label (R6.5)', () => {
  it('R6.5 an 8-minute bar is 48 px with no label, a 9-minute bar 54 px with none, a 10-minute bar 60 px with one', () => {
    const l = layoutTimeline(
      scheduleView(
        handWritten([
          { id: 'eight', start: -40, minutes: 8 },
          { id: 'nine', start: -30, minutes: 9 },
          { id: 'ten', start: -20, minutes: 10 },
        ]),
      ),
      WED_1930,
      'en-GB',
    );
    const widths = l.rows[0]?.bars.map((b) => [b.stepId, b.widthPx, b.showLabel]);
    expect(widths).toEqual([
      ['x.eight', 48, false],
      ['x.nine', 54, false],
      ['x.ten', 60, true],
    ]);
  });
});

describe('layoutTimeline on a rebuilt-shaped schedule (R6.3)', () => {
  it('R6.3 servingMin 15: the axis ends at 15 rounded up to a quarter hour and the serve line sits there', () => {
    const shifted: Schedule & { servingMin: number } = {
      ...H1_SCHEDULE,
      steps: H1_SCHEDULE.steps.map((s) => ({ ...s, startMin: s.startMin + 15, endMin: s.endMin + 15 })),
      servingMin: 15,
    };
    // chosen 19:30 is minute 0; the first start is minute -95 = 17:55, so the origin is 17:45 = -105.
    const l = layoutTimeline(scheduleView(shifted), WED_1930, 'en-GB');
    expect([l.originMin, l.endMin, l.widthPx]).toEqual([-105, 15, 720]);
    expect([l.serveLeftPx, l.serveLabel]).toEqual([720, 'Serve 19:45']);
    expect(l.ticks[0]?.label).toBe('17:45');
    expect(l.ticks[l.ticks.length - 1]?.label).toBe('19:45');
    expect(l.rows[0]?.bars[0]).toMatchObject({ stepId: 'chicken.prep', leftPx: 60, widthPx: 60 });
  });
});

p='src/web/components/timeline-layout.ts'
s=open(p).read()
a=s.index('export function layoutTimeline')
s=s[:a]+'''export function layoutTimeline(
  view: ScheduleView,
  chosenMs: number,
  locale?: string,
): TimelineLayout {
  const originMin = quarterFloor(chosenMs, view.startMin);
  const endMin = quarterCeil(chosenMs, view.servingMin);
  const x = (minute: number): number => (minute - originMin) * PX_PER_MIN;

  const ordered = [...view.steps].sort(
    (a, b) => a.startMin - b.startMin || compareIds(a.id, b.id),
  );

  const barOf = (s: ScheduledStep): TimelineBar | null => {
    const step = view.stepById.get(s.id);
    if (step === undefined) return null;
    const widthPx = (s.endMin - s.startMin) * PX_PER_MIN;
    return {
      stepId: s.id,
      title: step.title,
      leftPx: x(s.startMin),
      widthPx,
      fill: step.appliance === 'none' ? 'plain' : step.appliance,
      dashed: !step.handsOn,
      showLabel: widthPx >= LABEL_MIN_PX,
      critical: stepMark(s, step) === 'critical',
    };
  };

  const rows: TimelineRow[] = [];
  const resources: readonly (readonly [RowKind & ('cook' | 'burner' | 'oven'), string])[] = [
    ['cook', 'Cook'],
    ['burner', 'Burner'],
    ['oven', 'Oven'],
  ];
  for (const [kind] of resources) {
    const byLane = new Map<number, TimelineBar[]>();
    for (const s of ordered) {
      const lane = s.lanes[kind];
      const b = lane === null ? null : barOf(s);
      if (lane === null || b === null) continue;
      byLane.set(lane, [...(byLane.get(lane) ?? []), b]);
    }
    for (const lane of [...byLane.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
      rows.push({
        key: `${kind}-${lane}`,
        kind,
        lane,
        label: laneName(kind, lane),
        bars: byLane.get(lane) ?? [],
      });
    }
  }

  // Hands-free: no appliance and no cook. Each goes into the lowest row that is free at its start.
  const free: { end: number; bars: TimelineBar[] }[] = [];
  for (const s of ordered) {
    const step = view.stepById.get(s.id);
    if (step === undefined || step.appliance !== 'none' || step.handsOn) continue;
    const b = barOf(s);
    if (b === null) continue;
    const row = free.find((r) => r.end <= s.startMin);
    if (row === undefined) free.push({ end: s.endMin, bars: [b] });
    else {
      row.bars.push(b);
      row.end = s.endMin;
    }
  }
  for (const [index, row] of free.entries()) {
    rows.push({
      key: `hands-free-${index + 1}`,
      kind: 'hands-free',
      lane: index + 1,
      label: free.length === 1 ? COPY.handsFree : `${COPY.handsFree} ${index + 1}`,
      bars: row.bars,
    });
  }

  const ticks: TimelineTick[] = [];
  for (let minute = originMin; minute <= endMin; minute += 15) {
    const ms = clockAt(chosenMs, minute);
    const local = new Date(ms);
    const clock = formatClock(ms, locale);
    const midnight = local.getHours() === 0 && local.getMinutes() === 0;
    ticks.push({
      minute,
      leftPx: x(minute),
      label: midnight ? `${formatWeekday(ms, locale)} ${clock}` : clock,
    });
  }

  return {
    originMin,
    endMin,
    widthPx: x(endMin),
    rows,
    ticks,
    serveLeftPx: x(view.servingMin),
    serveLabel: serveLabel(formatClock(clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin), locale)),
  };
}
'''
s=s.replace("import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';","""import { compareIds } from '../../core/index.js';
import type { ScheduledStep } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY, laneName, serveLabel } from '../copy.js';
import { clockAt, formatClock, formatWeekday, quarterCeil, quarterFloor } from '../state/clock.js';
import { stepMark } from '../state/schedule-view.js';
import type { ScheduleView } from '../state/schedule-view.js';""")
open(p,'w').write(s)

/** The serve line's label: "Serve 19:30". */
export function serveLabel(time: string): string {
  return `Serve ${time}`;
}

[tool:execute FAILED] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/web/components/timeline-layout.pbt.ts <<'EOF'
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { schedule } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import { layoutTimeline } from '../../../src/web/components/timeline-layout.js';
import { scheduleView } from '../../../src/web/state/schedule-view.js';
import { arbPlanAndKitchen } from '../../support/arbitraries.js';
import { fcParams } from '../../support/fc-params.js';

const original = process.env['TZ'];
const CHOSEN_MS = Date.parse('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z');

describe('timeline layout properties', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    process.env['TZ'] = 'Europe/London';
    expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
  });
  afterAll(() => {
    if (original === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
    else process.env['TZ'] = original;
  });

  it('P-UI3 bars never overlap in a row, sit on exactly their lanes, hands-free rows equal peak overlap, pixels follow the formula', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(arbPlanAndKitchen(), ({ plan, kitchen }) => {
        const result = schedule(plan, kitchen);
        expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
        if (!result.ok) return;
        const s = result.value;
        const layout = layoutTimeline(scheduleView(s), CHOSEN_MS, 'en-GB');
        const first = Math.min(...s.steps.map((t) => t.startMin));

        // Axis: origin at most 14 minutes before the first start, end at most 14 after serving.
        expect(layout.originMin <= first && first - layout.originMin < 15).toBe(true);
        expect(layout.endMin >= 0 && layout.endMin < 15).toBe(true);
        expect(layout.widthPx).toBe((layout.endMin - layout.originMin) * 6);

        const byId = new Map(s.steps.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
        const stepOf = new Map(plan.steps.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
        const seen = new Map<string, string[]>();
        for (const row of layout.rows) {
          // No two bars of a row overlap (plain loop over pairs).
          for (let i = 0; i < row.bars.length; i += 1) {
            for (let j = i + 1; j < row.bars.length; j += 1) {
              const a = row.bars[i];
              const b = row.bars[j];
              if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error('index');
              expect(a.leftPx + a.widthPx <= b.leftPx || b.leftPx + b.widthPx <= a.leftPx).toBe(
                true,
              );
            }
          }
          for (const b of row.bars) {
            const t = byId.get(b.stepId);
            if (t === undefined) throw new Error('unknown step in a bar');
            expect(b.leftPx).toBe((t.startMin - layout.originMin) * 6);
            expect(b.widthPx).toBe((t.endMin - t.startMin) * 6);
            expect(b.showLabel).toBe(b.widthPx >= 56);
            seen.set(b.stepId, [...(seen.get(b.stepId) ?? []), row.label]);
          }
        }

        // Each step is on exactly the lanes it holds, or on one hands-free row.
        for (const t of s.steps) {
          const step = stepOf.get(t.id);
          if (step === undefined) throw new Error('unknown step');
          const expected: string[] = [];
          if (t.lanes.cook !== null) expected.push(`Cook ${t.lanes.cook}`);
          if (t.lanes.burner !== null) expected.push(`Burner ${t.lanes.burner}`);
          if (t.lanes.oven !== null) expected.push(`Oven ${t.lanes.oven}`);
          const got = seen.get(t.id) ?? [];
          if (expected.length > 0) expect(got.sort()).toEqual(expected.sort());
          else {
            expect(got).toHaveLength(1);
            expect(got[0]?.startsWith('Hands-free')).toBe(true);
          }
        }

        // Oracle: the number of hands-free rows is the peak number of hands-free steps active.
        const free = s.steps.filter((t) => {
          const step = stepOf.get(t.id);
          return step !== undefined && step.appliance === 'none' && !step.handsOn;
        });
        let peak = 0;
        for (let minute = first; minute < 0; minute += 1) {
          peak = Math.max(
            peak,
            free.filter((t) => t.startMin <= minute && minute < t.endMin).length,
          );
        }
        expect(layout.rows.filter((r) => r.kind === 'hands-free')).toHaveLength(peak);
      }),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/components/Timeline.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { hashOf } from '../state/route.js';
import type { TimelineBar, TimelineLayout } from './timeline-layout.js';

/** Moves focus to the plan list item of a step, or to the list itself (R6.7, R6.8). */
function focusElement(id: string): void {
  const target = document.getElementById(id);
  if (target === null) return;
  target.focus();
  target.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
}

function BarView(props: { bar: TimelineBar }): VNode {
  const { bar } = props;
  const classes = [
    'bar',
    `bar--${bar.fill}`,
    bar.dashed ? 'bar--dashed' : '',
    bar.critical ? 'bar--critical' : '',
  ]
    .filter((c) => c !== '')
    .join(' ');
  return (
    <div
      class={classes}
      data-step-id={bar.stepId}
      style={{ left: `${bar.leftPx}px`, width: `${bar.widthPx}px` }}
    >
      {bar.showLabel && (
        <span class="bar__label">
          {bar.critical ? '▲ ' : ''}
          {bar.title}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * The timeline: one row per lane. A picture only: `aria-hidden`, nothing focusable; every
 * bar's step is in the plan list, which a visible link skips to (R6.7). A tap on a bar
 * focuses its list item (R6.8) through one delegated handler.
 */
export function Timeline(props: { layout: TimelineLayout }): VNode {
  const { layout } = props;
  const onClick = (event: MouseEvent): void => {
    const target = event.target;
    if (!(target instanceof Element)) return;
    const bar = target.closest('[data-step-id]');
    const id = bar?.getAttribute('data-step-id');
    if (id !== null && id !== undefined) focusElement(`step-${id}`);
  };
  const innerStyle = { '--timeline-width': `${layout.widthPx}px` };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="timeline-heading" class="no-print">
      <h2 id="timeline-heading">{COPY.timelineHeading}</h2>
      <p class="timeline-skip">
        <a
          href={hashOf('plan')}
          onClick={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            focusElement('plan-list');
          }}
        >
          {COPY.skipToList}
        </a>
      </p>
      <div class="timeline" aria-hidden="true" onClick={onClick}>
        <div class="timeline__inner" style={innerStyle}>
          <div class="timeline__row timeline__row--axis">
            <div class="timeline__label" />
            <div class="timeline__track">
              {layout.ticks.map((tick, index) => (
                <span
                  key={tick.minute}
                  class={
                    index === layout.ticks.length - 1
                      ? 'timeline__tick timeline__tick--end'
                      : 'timeline__tick'
                  }
                  style={{ left: `${tick.leftPx}px` }}
                >
                  {tick.label}
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          {layout.rows.map((row) => (
            <div key={row.key} class="timeline__row">
              <div class="timeline__label">{row.label}</div>
              <div class="timeline__track">
                {row.bars.map((bar) => (
                  <BarView key={bar.stepId} bar={bar} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div class="timeline__row timeline__row--axis">
            <div class="timeline__label" />
            <div class="timeline__track">
              <span class="timeline__serve-label" style={{ left: `${layout.serveLeftPx}px` }}>
                {layout.serveLabel}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="timeline__serve-line" style={{ left: `${layout.serveLeftPx}px` }} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
EOF

## What changed
- **State:**
  - New `src/web/state/schedule-view.ts`.
  - `store.ts` gains the `kitchen/set`, `serving/set-time`, `serving/set-instant` and `plan/enter` actions, plus `sampleLoad(sample)`.
  - `hooks.ts` gains `useNow`.
- **Components:**
  - New `plan-list-items.ts`, `timeline-layout.ts`, `Stepper`, `KitchenControls`, `ServingTime`, `PlanSummary`, `WarningList`, `MissingAppliance`, `PlanList` and `Timeline`.
  - `Plan.tsx` is rewritten, and `SampleList.tsx` now uses `sampleLoad`.
- **Copy and styles:** `copy.ts` and `components.css` are extended.
- **Tests:**
  - New `plan-list-items.test.ts` and `.pbt.ts` (P-UI4), `timeline-layout.test.ts` and `.pbt.ts` (P-UI3), and `e2e/plan.spec.ts`.
  - `store.test.ts` is extended and `menu.spec.ts` updated.

## Sample title
The dinner's title now comes from `sampleLoad`, which copies the plan and sets `title` to the sample's title. The recording is untouched. The unit test is in `store.test.ts`, and `menu.spec.ts` now expects the sample title.

## Visual checks
I took screenshots at 390×844 in light and dark. The page never scrolled sideways and no target was under 48 px. Three things I changed:
- **Summary position:** "Start at 17:40" was cut off at the fold. The summary now comes right after the `<h1>`, before the Kitchen controls.
- **Plan list items:** the time range and the title ran together. The range now sits on its own line.
- **Critical mark:** "▲ Critical" broke across lines. It no longer wraps.

The timeline looks right: lane labels stay put while it scrolls, and the oven bar is hatched with a dashed accent outline.

## Decisions I made
- **9-minute bar:** the task says "54 px, label", but 9 × 6 = 54 is under the 56 px minimum in the design and R6.5. I followed 56 px, so 9 minutes has no label. The test also covers 8 minutes (48 px, no label) and 10 minutes (60 px, label).
- **Hold warning wording:** the design says "latest user's title", but the guarantees changed `wait` to the first user. I used the user that starts first.
- **Past-start note:** it shows when the start is before the current instant, not the current minute. That is what the 17:39:30 test needs.
- **Small bars:** the `▲` is drawn only on bars wide enough for a label. Narrower bars show a 3 px accent outline instead, since `▲` won't fit and the 16 px minimum applies. The list carries "Critical" in text.
- **Skip link:** it uses `href="#/plan"` with a script handler, so the fragment router isn't broken.
- **Plan opened without a serving time:** the Plan screen dispatches `plan/enter` itself, because the Plan link in the flow nav can reach it with none chosen.

## Not verified
- **Invalid-plan message:** the invalid-plan message with "Go to steps" is implemented but I couldn't test it. A plan with an error never reaches the Plan screen, because `reachable` and `loadState` redirect it to Steps. I tested that redirect instead. This conflicts with the T3.4 e2e line, but no requirement changes.
- **Properties:** P-UI3 and P-UI4 passed on their first run. They test code the examples had already driven, so I never saw them fail. The example tests did fail first, on assertions.
- **Sample-title test:** I wrote `sampleLoad` before its test, so I did not see that test fail first.
- **Other gaps:** I did not run axe on the Plan screen (that is T8.2), and I did not test the error boundary.

I did not touch `docs/kiro-evidence/`; its modified files in `git status` are not from me.

    /** The title of the step that uses `stepId`'s result first, or null for a terminal step. */
    function firstUserTitle(view: ScheduleView, stepId: string): string | null {
      const start = new Map(view.steps.map((s) => [s.id, s.startMin]));
      const users = view.plan.steps
        .filter((s) => s.after.includes(stepId))
        .sort((a, b) => (start.get(a.id) ?? 0) - (start.get(b.id) ?? 0) || compareIds(a.id, b.id));
      return users[0]?.title ?? null;
    }

/** Applies a plan edit; a refused edit or one that changes nothing leaves the state as it is. */
function applyEdit(
  state: AppState,
  edit: (plan: Plan) => Result<{ plan: Plan; notice: string | null }, unknown>,
): AppState {
  if (state.dinner === null) return state;
  const result = edit(state.dinner.plan);
  if (!result.ok || result.value.plan === state.dinner.plan) return state;
  return {
    ...state,
    dinner: { ...state.dinner, plan: result.value.plan },
    notice: result.value.notice,
  };
}

const ROUTE_ORDER""",1)
s=s.replace("""    default:
      return reduceCook""","""    case 'dish/add':
      return applyEdit(state, (plan) => addDish(plan, action.name));
    case 'dish/rename':
      return applyEdit(state, (plan) => renameDish(plan, action.index, action.name));
    case 'dish/remove':
      return applyEdit(state, (plan) => removeDish(plan, action.index));
    case 'step/add':
      return applyEdit(state, (plan) => addStep(plan, action.dishIndex));
    case 'step/update':
      return applyEdit(state, (plan) => updateStep(plan, action.index, action.patch));
    case 'step/remove':
      return applyEdit(state, (plan) => removeStep(plan, action.index));
    default:
      return reduceCook""")
s=s.replace("import type { StepPatch } from './plan-edit.js';","""import {
  addDish,
  addStep,
  removeDish,
  removeStep,
  renameDish,
  updateStep,
} from './plan-edit.js';
import type { StepPatch } from './plan-edit.js';""")

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat >> src/web/copy.ts <<'E'

// Steps screen (T5)
export const STEPS_COPY = {
  issuesHeading: 'Issues',
  noIssues: 'No issues.',
  fixErrorsToMake: 'Fix the errors to make the plan.',
  makePlan: 'Make the plan',
  error: 'Error',
  warning: 'Warning',
  noAppliance: 'No appliance',
  burner: 'Burner',
  oven: 'Oven',
  handsOn: 'Hands-on',
  handsFree: 'Hands-free',
  keeps: 'Keeps',
  waitsForNothing: 'Waits for nothing',
  cookingStarted: 'Cooking has started, so the plan cannot be changed here.',
} as const;

function count(n: number, one: string, many: string): string {
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
}

/** The intro under the Steps heading: "Sunday roast. Fix any errors, then make the plan." */
export function stepsIntro(title: string): string {
  return `${title}. Fix any errors, then make the plan.`;
}

/** "1 error and 0 warnings." */
export function issueSummary(errors: number, warnings: number): string {
  return `${count(errors, 'error', 'errors')} and ${count(warnings, 'warning', 'warnings')}.`;
}

/** The reason "Make the plan" is disabled: "Fix 1 error to make the plan." */
export function makeBlocked(errors: number): string {
  return `Fix ${count(errors, 'error', 'errors')} to make the plan.`;
}

export function applianceLabel(appliance: 'burner' | 'oven' | 'none'): string {
  if (appliance === 'burner') return STEPS_COPY.burner;
  if (appliance === 'oven') return STEPS_COPY.oven;
  return STEPS_COPY.noAppliance;
}

export function handsLabel(handsOn: boolean): string {
  return handsOn ? STEPS_COPY.handsOn : STEPS_COPY.handsFree;
}

/** "Keeps" or "Can wait 5 min". */
export function holdLabel(holdMin: number | null): string {
  return holdMin === null ? STEPS_COPY.keeps : `Can wait ${formatDuration(holdMin)}`;
}

/** "Waits for: Season and truss the chicken" or "Waits for nothing". */
export function waitsFor(titles: readonly string[]): string {
  return titles.length === 0 ? STEPS_COPY.waitsForNothing : `Waits for: ${titles.join(', ')}`;
}

cat > src/web/components/issue-placement.ts <<'E'
import { stepOrder } from '../state/plan-edit.js';
import type { Issue, Plan } from '../../core/index.js';

export interface PlacedIssue {
  /** Position in the issue list; the anchor is `issue-<n>`. */
  n: number;
  issue: Issue;
  /** True for the one copy that carries the anchor the summary links to. */
  anchor: boolean;
}

export interface IssuePlacement {
  /** Issues that name no step and no dish: shown in the summary only. */
  unplaced: PlacedIssue[];
  /** By dish id, shown at the top of the dish. */
  byDish: ReadonlyMap<string, PlacedIssue[]>;
  /** By step index, shown beside the step. */
  byStep: ReadonlyMap<number, PlacedIssue[]>;
}

function stepIdsOf(issue: Issue): string[] {
  if (issue.code === 'cycle') return issue.path;
  return 'stepId' in issue && issue.stepId !== undefined ? [issue.stepId] : [];
}

function dishIdOf(issue: Issue): string | null {
  return 'dishId' in issue && issue.dishId !== undefined ? issue.dishId : null;
}

/**
 * Where each issue is shown (R4.9): beside every step it names (every step of a cycle path),
 * at the top of the dish it names, else only in the summary. The anchor goes on the first
 * copy in screen order: dishes in plan order, steps in `stepOrder`.
 */
export function placeIssues(plan: Plan, issues: readonly Issue[]): IssuePlacement {
  const screenOrder = new Map<number, number>();
  let position = 0;
  const order = stepOrder(plan);
  for (const dish of plan.dishes) {
    for (const index of order) {
      if (plan.steps[index]?.dish === dish.id) screenOrder.set(index, position++);
    }
  }
  const byStep = new Map<number, PlacedIssue[]>();
  const byDish = new Map<string, PlacedIssue[]>();
  const unplaced: PlacedIssue[] = [];
  const push = <K>(map: Map<K, PlacedIssue[]>, key: K, placed: PlacedIssue): void => {
    map.set(key, [...(map.get(key) ?? []), placed]);
  };

  issues.forEach((issue, n) => {
    const ids = new Set(stepIdsOf(issue));
    const indices = plan.steps.flatMap((s, i) => (ids.has(s.id) ? [i] : []));
    if (indices.length > 0) {
      const first = indices.reduce((a, b) =>
        (screenOrder.get(b) ?? Infinity) < (screenOrder.get(a) ?? Infinity) ? b : a,
      );
      for (const index of indices) push(byStep, index, { n, issue, anchor: index === first });
      return;
    }
    const dishId = dishIdOf(issue);
    if (dishId !== null && plan.dishes.some((d) => d.id === dishId)) {
      push(byDish, dishId, { n, issue, anchor: true });
      return;
    }
    unplaced.push({ n, issue, anchor: false });
  });
  return { unplaced, byDish, byStep };
}

cat > src/web/components/IssueList.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { STEPS_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import type { Issue } from '../../core/index.js';
import type { PlacedIssue } from './issue-placement.js';

/** `!` and the word, so severity is never told by colour alone (R4.9). */
export function IssueMark(props: { issue: Issue }): VNode {
  const error = props.issue.severity === 'error';
  return (
    <span class={error ? 'mark mark--error' : 'mark mark--warn'}>
      <span aria-hidden="true">!</span> {error ? STEPS_COPY.error : STEPS_COPY.warning}
    </span>
  );
}

/** The issues beside a step or at the top of a dish. The anchored copy can take focus. */
export function IssueList(props: { items: readonly PlacedIssue[] }): VNode | null {
  if (props.items.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <ul class="issues">
      {props.items.map(({ n, issue, anchor }) => (
        <li
          key={n}
          class="issue"
          id={anchor ? `issue-${n}` : undefined}
          tabIndex={anchor ? -1 : undefined}
        >
          <IssueMark issue={issue} /> {issue.message}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

function jumpTo(n: number, event: Event): void {
  event.preventDefault();
  const target = document.getElementById(`issue-${n}`);
  if (target === null) return;
  target.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
  target.focus();
}

/** Every issue at the top of the screen, each linking to where it is shown (R4.9). */
export function IssueSummary(props: { items: readonly PlacedIssue[] }): VNode | null {
  if (props.items.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <ul class="issues">
      {props.items.map(({ n, issue }) => (
        <li key={n} class="summary-issue">
          <IssueMark issue={issue} />{' '}
          {issue.code === 'cycle' || 'stepId' in issue || 'dishId' in issue ? (
            <a href={`#issue-${n}`} onClick={(event) => jumpTo(n, event)}>
              {issue.message}
            </a>
          ) : (
            issue.message
          )}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/StepCard.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import type { Plan, Step } from '../../core/index.js';
import { applianceLabel, handsLabel, holdLabel, waitsFor } from '../copy.js';
import { formatDuration } from '../state/clock.js';
import { IssueList } from './IssueList.js';
import type { PlacedIssue } from './issue-placement.js';

interface Props {
  plan: Plan;
  step: Step;
  issues: readonly PlacedIssue[];
}

/** A step as a summary: title, id, minutes, appliance, hands-on, hold, prerequisites (R4.2). */
export function StepCard(props: Props): VNode {
  const { plan, step } = props;
  const titles = step.after.map((id) => plan.steps.find((s) => s.id === id)?.title ?? id);
  return (
    <li class="card step-card" data-step-id={step.id}>
      <h3>{step.title}</h3>
      <ul class="facts">
        <li class="mono">{step.id}</li>
        <li class="mono">{formatDuration(step.minutes)}</li>
        <li>{applianceLabel(step.appliance)}</li>
        <li>{handsLabel(step.handsOn)}</li>
        <li>{holdLabel(step.holdMin)}</li>
      </ul>
      <p class="muted">{waitsFor(titles)}</p>
      <IssueList items={props.issues} />
    </li>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/DishSection.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import type { Plan } from '../../core/index.js';
import { IssueList } from './IssueList.js';
import type { IssuePlacement } from './issue-placement.js';
import { StepCard } from './StepCard.js';

interface Props {
  plan: Plan;
  dishIndex: number;
  /** Step indices in screen order. */
  order: readonly number[];
  placement: IssuePlacement;
}

/** A dish: its name, its own issues, then its steps in dependency order (R4.1). */
export function DishSection(props: Props): VNode | null {
  const { plan, placement } = props;
  const dish = plan.dishes[props.dishIndex];
  if (dish === undefined) return null;
  const headingId = `dish-${props.dishIndex}-heading`;
  return (
    <section class="dish" aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <h2 id={headingId}>{dish.name}</h2>
      <IssueList items={placement.byDish.get(dish.id) ?? []} />
      <ol class="card-list step-list">
        {props.order.map((index) => {
          const step = plan.steps[index];
          if (step === undefined || step.dish !== dish.id) return null;
          return (
            <StepCard
              key={index}
              plan={plan}
              step={step}
              issues={placement.byStep.get(index) ?? []}
            />
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}
