cat > src/web/screens/Steps.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { hasErrors } from '../../core/index.js';
import { DishSection } from '../components/DishSection.js';
import { IssueSummary } from '../components/IssueList.js';
import { placeIssues } from '../components/issue-placement.js';
import { COPY, issueSummary, makeBlocked, STEPS_COPY, stepsIntro } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState, useClock, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { stepOrder } from '../state/plan-edit.js';
import { selectCheck } from '../state/store.js';

/** Parsed steps per dish, issues beside them, and the way on to the plan (R4). */
export function Steps(): VNode {
  const dinner = useAppState((state) => state.dinner);
  const check = useAppState(selectCheck);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();

  if (dinner === null || check === null) {
    return (
      <main class="screen">
        <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{COPY.stepsHeading}</h1>
      </main>
    );
  }
  const { plan } = dinner;
  const errors = check.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error').length;
  const warnings = check.issues.length - errors;
  const placement = placeIssues(plan, check.issues);
  const order = stepOrder(plan);
  const placedSummary = check.issues.map((issue, n) => ({ n, issue, anchor: false }));
  const blocked = hasErrors(check.issues);

  return (
    <main class="screen">
      <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{COPY.stepsHeading}</h1>
      <p>{stepsIntro(plan.title)}</p>
      <section id="issues-summary" aria-labelledby="issues-heading">
        <h2 id="issues-heading">{STEPS_COPY.issuesHeading}</h2>
        <p>
          {check.issues.length === 0 ? STEPS_COPY.noIssues : issueSummary(errors, warnings)}
          {errors > 0 && ` ${STEPS_COPY.fixErrorsToMake}`}
        </p>
        <IssueSummary items={placedSummary} />
      </section>
      {plan.dishes.map((dish, dishIndex) => (
        <DishSection
          key={dish.id}
          plan={plan}
          dishIndex={dishIndex}
          order={order}
          placement={placement}
        />
      ))}
      <div class="actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button button--primary"
          disabled={blocked}
          aria-describedby={blocked ? 'make-blocked' : undefined}
          onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'plan/enter', nowMs: clock.nowMs() })}
        >
          {STEPS_COPY.makePlan}
        </button>
      </div>
      {blocked && (
        <p id="make-blocked" class="muted">
          {makeBlocked(errors)}
        </p>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

// Step editor (T5.5)
export const EDITOR_COPY = {
  edit: 'Edit',
  doneEditing: 'Done editing',
  title: 'Title',
  minutes: 'Minutes',
  appliance: 'Appliance',
  handsOn: 'Hands-on (needs a cook the whole time)',
  dish: 'Dish',
  keeps: 'Keeps (can wait as long as needed)',
  canWaitUpTo: 'Can wait up to (minutes)',
  waitsFor: 'Waits for',
  alreadyWaits: '(already waits for this step)',
  notAStep: '(not a step in this plan)',
  thisStep: '(this step)',
  titleError: 'Give the step a title.',
  minutesError: 'Enter whole minutes from 1 to 480.',
  holdError: 'Enter whole minutes from 0 to 480.',
} as const;

cat > src/web/components/PrerequisiteChecklist.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useId } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { Plan } from '../../core/index.js';
import { EDITOR_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { stepOrder, waitingOn } from '../state/plan-edit.js';

interface Props {
  plan: Plan;
  /** Index of the step being edited. */
  index: number;
  onChange: (after: string[]) => void;
}

interface Option {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  suffix: string | null;
  checked: boolean;
  disabled: boolean;
}

/** The options of the checklist: other steps (same dish first), then ids that are not steps (R4.3, R4.5). */
function optionsOf(plan: Plan, index: number): Option[] {
  const step = plan.steps[index];
  if (step === undefined) return [];
  const later = waitingOn(plan, step.id);
  const order = stepOrder(plan).filter((i) => i !== index);
  const sameDish = order.filter((i) => plan.steps[i]?.dish === step.dish);
  const others = plan.dishes
    .filter((d) => d.id !== step.dish)
    .flatMap((d) => order.filter((i) => plan.steps[i]?.dish === d.id));
  const options: Option[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const i of [...sameDish, ...others]) {
    const other = plan.steps[i];
    if (other === undefined || other.id === step.id || seen.has(other.id)) continue;
    seen.add(other.id);
    const checked = step.after.includes(other.id);
    // A step that already waits for this one would make a cycle, unless it is checked and
    // the cook needs to be able to clear it.
    const disabled = later.has(other.id) && !checked;
    options.push({
      id: other.id,
      label: other.title,
      suffix: disabled ? EDITOR_COPY.alreadyWaits : null,
      checked,
      disabled,
    });
  }
  const stepIds = new Set(plan.steps.map((s) => s.id));
  for (const id of new Set(step.after)) {
    if (id === step.id) {
      options.push({ id, label: id, suffix: EDITOR_COPY.thisStep, checked: true, disabled: false });
    } else if (!stepIds.has(id)) {
      options.push({ id, label: id, suffix: EDITOR_COPY.notAStep, checked: true, disabled: false });
    }
  }
  return options;
}

/** "Waits for": a checklist of the other steps; options that would make a cycle are disabled. */
export function PrerequisiteChecklist(props: Props): VNode | null {
  const step = props.plan.steps[props.index];
  const name = useId();
  if (step === undefined) return null;
  const toggle = (id: string, checked: boolean): void => {
    props.onChange(
      checked ? [...step.after.filter((a) => a !== id), id] : step.after.filter((a) => a !== id),
    );
  };
  return (
    <fieldset class="checklist">
      <legend>{EDITOR_COPY.waitsFor}</legend>
      {optionsOf(props.plan, props.index).map((option) => (
        <label key={option.id} class={`check${option.disabled ? ' check--off' : ''}`}>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            name={name}
            checked={option.checked}
            disabled={option.disabled}
            onChange={(event) => toggle(option.id, event.currentTarget.checked)}
          />
          <span>{option.suffix === null ? option.label : `${option.label} ${option.suffix}`}</span>
        </label>
      ))}
    </fieldset>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/StepEditor.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { Appliance, Plan } from '../../core/index.js';
import { EDITOR_COPY, STEPS_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { DEFAULT_HOLD_MIN } from '../state/plan-edit.js';
import type { StepPatch } from '../state/plan-edit.js';
import { PrerequisiteChecklist } from './PrerequisiteChecklist.js';

interface Props {
  plan: Plan;
  index: number;
  onDone: () => void;
}

/** Parses whole minutes in a range; null when the text is not that. */
function wholeMinutes(text: string, min: number, max: number): number | null {
  if (!/^\d{1,4}$/.test(text.trim())) return null;
  const value = Number(text.trim());
  return value >= min && value <= max ? value : null;
}

/**
 * Edits one step. Field text is local; a value goes to the store only when it would pass
 * `StepSchema`, so the stored plan stays valid and a bad value keeps what was typed (R4.4).
 */
export function StepEditor(props: Props): VNode | null {
  const step = props.plan.steps[props.index];
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const ids = useId();
  const titleInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(step?.title ?? '');
  const [minutes, setMinutes] = useState(String(step?.minutes ?? ''));
  const [hold, setHold] = useState(String(step?.holdMin ?? DEFAULT_HOLD_MIN));
  const [titleError, setTitleError] = useState(false);
  const [minutesError, setMinutesError] = useState(false);
  const [holdError, setHoldError] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    titleInput.current?.focus();
  }, []);

  if (step === undefined) return null;
  const update = (patch: StepPatch): void =>
    dispatch({ type: 'step/update', index: props.index, patch });
  const keeps = step.holdMin === null;

  return (
    <div class="step-editor">
      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-title`}>{EDITOR_COPY.title}</label>
        <input
          id={`${ids}-title`}
          ref={titleInput}
          class="input"
          type="text"
          value={title}
          aria-invalid={titleError}
          aria-describedby={titleError ? `${ids}-title-error` : undefined}
          onInput={(event) => {
            const value = event.currentTarget.value;
            setTitle(value);
            const valid = value.trim() !== '';
            setTitleError(!valid);
            if (valid) update({ title: value.trim() });
          }}
        />
        {titleError && (
          <p id={`${ids}-title-error`} class="field-error">
            {EDITOR_COPY.titleError}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-minutes`}>{EDITOR_COPY.minutes}</label>
        <input
          id={`${ids}-minutes`}
          class="input input--mono"
          type="text"
          inputMode="numeric"
          value={minutes}
          aria-invalid={minutesError}
          aria-describedby={minutesError ? `${ids}-minutes-error` : undefined}
          onInput={(event) => {
            const text = event.currentTarget.value;
            setMinutes(text);
            const value = wholeMinutes(text, 1, 480);
            setMinutesError(value === null);
            if (value !== null) update({ minutes: value });
          }}
        />
        {minutesError && (
          <p id={`${ids}-minutes-error`} class="field-error">
            {EDITOR_COPY.minutesError}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-appliance`}>{EDITOR_COPY.appliance}</label>
        <select
          id={`${ids}-appliance`}
          class="input"
          value={step.appliance}
          onChange={(event) => update({ appliance: event.currentTarget.value as Appliance })}
        >
          <option value="burner">{STEPS_COPY.burner}</option>
          <option value="oven">{STEPS_COPY.oven}</option>
          <option value="none">{STEPS_COPY.noAppliance}</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <label class="check">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={step.handsOn}
          onChange={(event) => update({ handsOn: event.currentTarget.checked })}
        />
        <span>{EDITOR_COPY.handsOn}</span>
      </label>

      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-dish`}>{EDITOR_COPY.dish}</label>
        <select
          id={`${ids}-dish`}
          class="input"
          value={step.dish}
          onChange={(event) => update({ dish: event.currentTarget.value })}
        >
          {props.plan.dishes.map((dish) => (
            <option key={dish.id} value={dish.id}>
              {dish.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      <label class="check">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={keeps}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setHoldError(false);
            if (event.currentTarget.checked) update({ holdMin: null });
            else {
              setHold(String(DEFAULT_HOLD_MIN));
              update({ holdMin: DEFAULT_HOLD_MIN });
            }
          }}
        />
        <span>{EDITOR_COPY.keeps}</span>
      </label>

      {!keeps && (
        <div class="field">
          <label for={`${ids}-hold`}>{EDITOR_COPY.canWaitUpTo}</label>
          <input
            id={`${ids}-hold`}
            class="input input--mono"
            type="text"
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={hold}
            aria-invalid={holdError}
            aria-describedby={holdError ? `${ids}-hold-error` : undefined}
            onInput={(event) => {
              const text = event.currentTarget.value;
              setHold(text);
              const value = wholeMinutes(text, 0, 480);
              setHoldError(value === null);
              if (value !== null) update({ holdMin: value });
            }}
          />
          {holdError && (
            <p id={`${ids}-hold-error`} class="field-error">
              {EDITOR_COPY.holdError}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <PrerequisiteChecklist
        plan={props.plan}
        index={props.index}
        onChange={(after) => update({ after })}
      />

      <div class="actions">
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={props.onDone}>
          {EDITOR_COPY.doneEditing}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'E'

test.describe('add and remove', () => {

  }) => {
    await openSteps(page);

    await roast.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove step' }).click();
    await expect(roast).toContainText(
      'Remove \u201cRoast the chicken\u201d? 2 steps wait for it: Rest the chicken under foil, Make a roux in the roasting tin. They will no longer wait for it.',
    );

    await roast.getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep step' }).click();
    await expect(roast.getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep step' })).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(roast).toContainText('Waits for: Season and truss the chicken');
    await expect(roast.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove step' })).toBeFocused();
    await expect(page.getByRole('status')).toHaveText('');

    await roast.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove step' }).click();
    await roast.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove step' }).click();
    await expect(card(page, 'chicken.roast')).toHaveCount(0);

  });

    page,

    await expect(card(page, 'creme-brulee.step-1')).toContainText('creme-brulee.step-1');
    // The new step opens for editing with focus in its title.
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Title', { exact: true })).toBeFocused();
  });

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add dish' }).click();

    await expect(card(page, 'gravy.roux')).toBeVisible();

  test('R4.6 Add a step adds a New step to the dish and opens it', async ({ page }) => {

    await dish(page, 'Gravy').getByRole('button', { name: 'Add a step' }).click();

    await expect(page.getByLabel('Title', { exact: true })).toBeFocused();
  });

  test('R4.6 removing a dish asks and then removes its steps', async ({ page }) => {
    await openSteps(page);
    const greens = dish(page, 'Spring greens');
    await greens.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove dish' }).click();
    await expect(greens).toContainText('Remove the dish Spring greens and its 3 steps?');
    await greens.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove dish' }).click();
    await expect(dish(page, 'Spring greens')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByRole('status')).toHaveText('Removed the dish Spring greens.');
  });

  test('R4.8 the last step cannot be removed and says why', async ({ page }) => {
    const only: Plan = {
      title: 'One step',
      dishes: [{ id: 'toast', name: 'Toast' }],
      steps: [
        {
          id: 'toast.make',
          dish: 'toast',
          title: 'Make toast',
          minutes: 3,
          appliance: 'none',
          handsOn: true,
          after: [],
          holdMin: null,
        },
      ],
    };
    await openSteps(page, only);
    const remove = card(page, 'toast.make').getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove step' });
    await expect(remove).toBeDisabled();
    await expect(card(page, 'toast.make')).toContainText('A plan needs at least one step.');

    ).toBeDisabled();
    await expect(dish(page, 'Toast')).toContainText('A plan needs at least one dish.');
  });
});

[tool:execute FAILED] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat >> src/web/copy.ts <<'E'

// Add, rename and remove (T5.6)
export const MANAGE_COPY = {
  removeStep: 'Remove step',
  keepStep: 'Keep step',
  removeDish: 'Remove dish',
  keepDish: 'Keep dish',
  renameDish: 'Rename dish',
  dishName: 'Dish name',
  saveName: 'Save name',
  addStep: 'Add a step',
  addDish: 'Add a dish',
  addDishConfirm: 'Add dish',
  nameError: 'Give the dish a name.',
} as const;

function dependentsSentence(titles: readonly string[]): string {
  if (titles.length === 0) return '';
  const list = titles.join(', ');
  return titles.length === 1
    ? ` 1 step waits for it: ${list}. It will no longer wait for it.`
    : ` ${titles.length} steps wait for it: ${list}. They will no longer wait for it.`;
}

/** The question before a step is removed; `dependentTitles` are the steps that wait for it. */
export function removeStepAsk(title: string, dependentTitles: readonly string[]): string {
  return `Remove \u201c${title}\u201d?${dependentsSentence(dependentTitles)}`;
}

/** The question before a dish and its steps are removed. */
export function removeDishAsk(
  name: string,
  stepCount: number,
  dependentTitles: readonly string[],
): string {
  return `Remove the dish ${name} and its ${count(stepCount, 'step', 'steps')}?${dependentsSentence(dependentTitles)}`;
}

cat > src/web/components/DishNameForm.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { COPY, MANAGE_COPY } from '../copy.js';

interface Props {
  initial: string;
  submitLabel: string;
  onSubmit: (name: string) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

/** A dish name field with a submit and a cancel button; a blank name shows the error (R4.6). */
export function DishNameForm(props: Props): VNode {
  const ids = useId();
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState(props.initial);
  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    input.current?.focus();
  }, []);
  const submit = (event: Event): void => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (name.trim() === '') setError(true);
    else props.onSubmit(name);
  };
  return (
    <form class="name-form" onSubmit={submit}>
      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-name`}>{MANAGE_COPY.dishName}</label>
        <input
          id={`${ids}-name`}
          ref={input}
          class="input"
          type="text"
          maxLength={80}
          value={name}
          aria-invalid={error}
          aria-describedby={error ? `${ids}-error` : undefined}
          onInput={(event) => {
            setName(event.currentTarget.value);
            setError(false);
          }}
        />
        {error && (
          <p id={`${ids}-error`} class="field-error">
            {MANAGE_COPY.nameError}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      <div class="actions">
        <button type="submit" class="button button--primary">
          {props.submitLabel}
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={props.onCancel}>
          {COPY.cancel}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/DishSection.tsx <<'E'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { Plan } from '../../core/index.js';
import { EDIT_ERRORS, MANAGE_COPY, removeDishAsk } from '../copy.js';
import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { dependentsOf } from '../state/plan-edit.js';
import { ConfirmInline } from './ConfirmInline.js';
import { DishNameForm } from './DishNameForm.js';
import { IssueList } from './IssueList.js';
import type { IssuePlacement } from './issue-placement.js';
import { StepCard } from './StepCard.js';

interface Props {
  plan: Plan;
  dishIndex: number;
  /** Step indices in screen order. */
  order: readonly number[];
  placement: IssuePlacement;
  /** Index of the step whose editor is open, if any. */
  editing: number | null;
  readOnly: boolean;
  onEdit: (index: number | null) => void;
}

/** Puts focus on `id` after the render that follows the current dispatch. */
function focusAfterRender(id: string): void {
  queueMicrotask(() => document.getElementById(id)?.focus());
}

/** A dish: its name, its own issues, then its steps in dependency order (R4.1, R4.6). */
export function DishSection(props: Props): VNode | null {
  const { plan, placement } = props;
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const reasonId = useId();
  const renameButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const removeButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const wasRenaming = useRef(false);
  const wasConfirming = useRef(false);
  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState(false);
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (wasRenaming.current && !renaming) renameButton.current?.focus();
    wasRenaming.current = renaming;
  }, [renaming]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (wasConfirming.current && !confirming) removeButton.current?.focus();
    wasConfirming.current = confirming;
  }, [confirming]);

  const dish = plan.dishes[props.dishIndex];
  if (dish === undefined) return null;
  const headingId = `dish-${props.dishIndex}-heading`;
  const stepIds = plan.steps.filter((s) => s.dish === dish.id).map((s) => s.id);
  const lastDish = plan.dishes.length === 1;
  const lastStep = stepIds.length === plan.steps.length;
  const removeReason = lastDish ? EDIT_ERRORS.last_dish : lastStep ? EDIT_ERRORS.last_step : null;
  const stepsFull = plan.steps.length >= 80;

  return (
    <section class="dish" aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <h2 id={headingId} tabIndex={-1}>
        {dish.name}
      </h2>
      <IssueList items={placement.byDish.get(dish.id) ?? []} />
      {!props.readOnly && renaming && (
        <DishNameForm
          initial={dish.name}
          submitLabel={MANAGE_COPY.saveName}
          onSubmit={(name) => {
            dispatch({ type: 'dish/rename', index: props.dishIndex, name });
            setRenaming(false);
          }}
          onCancel={() => setRenaming(false)}
        />
      )}
      {!props.readOnly && confirming && (
        <ConfirmInline
          text={removeDishAsk(
            dish.name,
            stepIds.length,
            dependentsOf(plan, stepIds).map((i) => plan.steps[i]?.title ?? ''),
          )}
          confirmLabel={MANAGE_COPY.removeDish}
          cancelLabel={MANAGE_COPY.keepDish}
          onConfirm={() => {
            props.onEdit(null);
            dispatch({ type: 'dish/remove', index: props.dishIndex });
            focusAfterRender('issues-heading');
          }}
          onCancel={() => setConfirming(false)}
        />
      )}
      {!props.readOnly && !renaming && !confirming && (
        <div class="actions">
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button"
            ref={renameButton}
            onClick={() => setRenaming(true)}
          >
            {MANAGE_COPY.renameDish}
            <span class="visually-hidden"> {dish.name}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button"
            ref={removeButton}
            disabled={removeReason !== null}
            aria-describedby={removeReason === null ? undefined : reasonId}
            onClick={() => setConfirming(true)}
          >
            {MANAGE_COPY.removeDish}
            <span class="visually-hidden"> {dish.name}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {!props.readOnly && removeReason !== null && (
        <p id={reasonId} class="muted">
          {removeReason}
        </p>
      )}
      <ol class="card-list step-list">
        {props.order.map((index) => {
          const step = plan.steps[index];
          if (step === undefined || step.dish !== dish.id) return null;
          return (
            <StepCard
              key={index}
              plan={plan}
              step={step}
              index={index}
              issues={placement.byStep.get(index) ?? []}
              editing={props.editing === index}
              readOnly={props.readOnly}
              onEdit={() => props.onEdit(index)}
              onDone={() => props.onEdit(null)}
              onRemove={() => {
                props.onEdit(null);
                dispatch({ type: 'step/remove', index });
                focusAfterRender(headingId);
              }}
            />
          );
        })}
      </ol>
      {!props.readOnly && (
        <div class="actions">
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button"
            disabled={stepsFull}
            aria-describedby={stepsFull ? `${reasonId}-steps` : undefined}
            onClick={() => {
              dispatch({ type: 'step/add', dishIndex: props.dishIndex });
              props.onEdit(plan.steps.length);
            }}
          >
            {MANAGE_COPY.addStep}
            <span class="visually-hidden"> {dish.name}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {!props.readOnly && stepsFull && (
        <p id={`${reasonId}-steps`} class="muted">
          {EDIT_ERRORS.too_many_steps}
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

## What I looked at

- the Steps screen with an error
- an open editor

- the add-dish form

There is no horizontal page scroll (scroll width equals client width at 390).

I changed one thing: the step id was running into the facts line, so it now has its own line. I deleted the scratch spec afterwards.

## Decisions I made
- **Focus in the checklist:** a prerequisite option that already waits for the step is disabled, unless it is already checked. A checked one stays enabled so an existing cycle can be fixed. An unknown id disappears from the checklist once unchecked.
- **Dependents wording:** the confirmation says "1 step waits for it: X. It will no longer wait for it." for one dependent. Two or more use the design's plural wording.
- **Dish removal notice:** it reuses the "no longer wait for it" sentence for dependents.
- **Anchors:** summary issue links use `href="#issue-n"` with `preventDefault` and focus the issue directly, so they don't trigger the hash router.
- **New helpers:** `issue-placement.ts` and `DishNameForm.tsx` are not in the design's file table. I added them to avoid duplicated logic.
- **Dish heading and Edit buttons:** the dish heading and the "Issues" heading take focus after a removal. Edit and Remove buttons carry the step title as visually hidden text so each name is unique.
- **Duplicate ids:** `ConfirmInline` now uses `useId`, so two confirmations can't produce duplicate ids.

## Not verified or not done
- Axe on the Steps screen is T8.2 and the 48 px target audit is T8.2; neither was run here. I only checked the sizes by eye.
- Cooking read-only mode (R4.12) is covered at reducer level only. `cook` is always null until cooking-mode lands, so the UI branch is unexercised.
- Pre-existing, noticed only: `public/assets/main.js` is 525 kB.

## Open question
A step whose `dish` matches no dish (for example from a Dinnerfile with an `unknown_dish` error) is not rendered in any dish section, so it can't be edited. R4.1 only specifies one section per dish. Do you want a fallback section for such steps?

p='test/web/state/api.test.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("import { fetchSamples } from","import { fetchSamples, postParse } from")
s+='''
/** A fake fetch that records the call and answers with a fixed response. */
function recording(
  status: number,
  body: string,
  headers: Record<string, string> = {},
): { fetchFn: FetchLike; calls: { url: string; init: RequestInit | undefined }[] } {
  const calls: { url: string; init: RequestInit | undefined }[] = [];
  const fetchFn: FetchLike = (url, init) => {
    calls.push({ url, init });
    return Promise.resolve(new Response(body, { status, headers }));
  };
  return { fetchFn, calls };
}

function errorBody(code: string): string {
  return JSON.stringify({ error: { code, message: 'plain English' } });
}

const NOW_MS = Date.parse('2026-11-11T17:00:00Z');
const signal = new AbortController().signal;

describe('postParse (R2.3, R2.5 to R2.7)', () => {
  it('R2.3 posts { text } as typed, as application/json, to /api/parse', async () => {
    const ok200 = JSON.stringify({ plan: SUNDAY_ROAST, issues: [], source: 'model' });
    const { fetchFn, calls } = recording(200, ok200);
    await postParse(fetchFn, '  Roast it.\\r\\n', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(calls[0]?.url).toBe('/api/parse');
    expect(calls[0]?.init?.method).toBe('POST');
    expect(new Headers(calls[0]?.init?.headers).get('content-type')).toBe('application/json');
    expect(calls[0]?.init?.body).toBe('{"text":"  Roast it.\\\\r\\\\n"}');
    expect(calls[0]?.init?.signal).toBe(signal);
  });

  it('R2.5 a 200 with a plan gives the plan', async () => {
    const body = JSON.stringify({ plan: SUNDAY_ROAST, issues: [], source: 'recording' });
    const result = await postParse(recording(200, body).fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    expect(result.value.title).toBe('Sunday roast');
    expect(result.value.steps).toHaveLength(SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.length);
  });

  it.each([
    'bad_request',
    'input_too_long',
    'model_unavailable',
    'model_refused',
    'model_output_invalid',
    'internal',
  ])('R2.6 code %s maps to its own failure kind', async (code) => {
    const result = await postParse(recording(502, errorBody(code)).fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: code } });
  });

  it.each(['not_found', 'invalid_plan', 'something_new'])(
    'R2.7 code %s is unexpected with the status',
    async (code) => {
      const result = await postParse(recording(422, errorBody(code)).fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
      expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'unexpected', status: 422 } });
    },
  );

  it('R2.6 rate_limited with Retry-After 120 gives now + 120 s', async () => {
    const { fetchFn } = recording(429, errorBody('rate_limited'), { 'Retry-After': '120' });
    const result = await postParse(fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'rate_limited', retryAtMs: NOW_MS + 120_000 },
    });
  });

  it('R2.6 daily_cap_reached with Retry-After 0 gives now', async () => {
    const { fetchFn } = recording(429, errorBody('daily_cap_reached'), { 'Retry-After': '0' });
    const result = await postParse(fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'daily_cap_reached', retryAtMs: NOW_MS },
    });
  });

  it('R2.6 a Retry-After that is not a whole number gives null', async () => {
    const { fetchFn } = recording(429, errorBody('rate_limited'), { 'Retry-After': 'abc' });
    const result = await postParse(fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'rate_limited', retryAtMs: null } });
  });

  it('R2.6 a missing Retry-After gives null', async () => {
    const result = await postParse(
      recording(429, errorBody('rate_limited')).fetchFn,
      'x',
      signal,
      NOW_MS,
    );
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'rate_limited', retryAtMs: null } });
  });

  it('R2.7 a 200 that fails the schema is unexpected', async () => {
    const result = await postParse(
      recording(200, JSON.stringify({ plan: { title: 'x' } })).fetchFn,
      'x',
      signal,
      NOW_MS,
    );
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'unexpected', status: 200 } });
  });

  it('R2.7 a non-JSON error body is unexpected', async () => {
    const result = await postParse(recording(500, '<html>').fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS);
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'unexpected', status: 500 } });
  });

  it('R2.7 a thrown fetch is network', async () => {
    const fetchFn: FetchLike = () => Promise.reject(new TypeError('Failed to fetch'));
    expect(await postParse(fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'network' },
    });
  });

  it('R2.4 an AbortError is cancelled', async () => {
    const fetchFn: FetchLike = () =>
      Promise.reject(new DOMException('The operation was aborted.', 'AbortError'));
    expect(await postParse(fetchFn, 'x', signal, NOW_MS)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'cancelled' },
    });
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/web/state/api.ts'
s=open(p).read()
i=s.index("/** `POST /api/parse` (R2.3). Placeholder")
s=s[:i]+'''const DIRECT_CODES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
  'bad_request',
  'input_too_long',
  'model_unavailable',
  'model_refused',
  'model_output_invalid',
  'internal',
]);

/** `Retry-After` as whole seconds >= 0 becomes an instant; anything else is null. */
function retryAt(header: string | null, nowMs: number): number | null {
  if (header === null || !/^[0-9]{1,9}$/.test(header.trim())) return null;
  return nowMs + Number(header.trim()) * 1000;
}

function isAbort(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError';
}

/**
 * `POST /api/parse` (R2.3). The text goes as typed; the server normalises it. Never throws.
 * A non-2xx body is mapped by its `code` (tech.md); the response's `issues` are not used.
 */
export async function postParse(
  fetchFn: FetchLike,
  text: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  nowMs: number,
): Promise<ParseOutcome> {
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetchFn('/api/parse', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ text }),
      signal,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return isAbort(error)
      ? { ok: false, error: { kind: 'cancelled' } }
      : err({ kind: 'network' });
  }
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    body = await response.json();
  } catch (error) {
    if (isAbort(error)) return { ok: false, error: { kind: 'cancelled' } };
    return err({ kind: 'unexpected', status: response.status });
  }
  if (response.ok) {
    const parsed = ParseResponseSchema.safeParse(body);
    return parsed.success
      ? ok(parsed.data.plan)
      : err({ kind: 'unexpected', status: response.status });
  }
  const failure = ApiErrorBodySchema.safeParse(body);
  const unexpected = err({ kind: 'unexpected', status: response.status } as const);
  if (!failure.success) return unexpected;
  const code = failure.data.error.code;
  if (code === 'rate_limited' || code === 'daily_cap_reached') {
    return err({ kind: code, retryAtMs: retryAt(response.headers.get('Retry-After'), nowMs) });
  }
  if (DIRECT_CODES.has(code)) {
    return err({ kind: code } as ParseFailure);
  }
  return unexpected;
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat >> e2e/support.ts <<'EOF'

/** Answers `POST /api/parse` with a fixed status, JSON body and headers; the request body is returned. */
export async function fakeParse(
  page: Page,
  status: number,
  body: unknown,
  headers: Record<string, string> = {},
): Promise<{ lastBody: () => unknown }> {
  let last: unknown = null;
  await page.route('**/api/parse', async (route) => {
    last = route.request().postDataJSON();
    await route.fulfill({
      status,
      contentType: 'application/json',
      headers,
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  });
  return { lastBody: () => last };
}

/** A parse error body as the server sends it (tech.md). */
export function parseError(code: string): { error: { code: string; message: string } } {
  return { error: { code, message: 'Plain English from the server.' } };
}
EOF

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import { FIXED_NOW, fakeParse, parseError } from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
});

const OK_BODY = { plan: SUNDAY_ROAST, issues: [], source: 'model' };

const TOO_LONG = 'The recipe text is over 12,000 characters. Shorten it or split it into two dinners.';
const MEANWHILE = 'Meanwhile, sample dinners and Dinnerfiles still work.';

test.describe('paste recipes', () => {
  test('R2.1 the counter starts at 0 of 12,000 and follows the text', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page.getByText('0 of 12,000 characters')).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast it.');
    await expect(page.getByText('9 of 12,000 characters')).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('R2.2 empty text disables "Read recipes" and says why', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' })).toBeDisabled();
    await expect(page.getByText('Paste at least one recipe.')).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('R2.2 12,001 characters disable "Read recipes" with the too-long copy', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('a'.repeat(12_001));
    await expect(page.getByText('12,001 of 12,000 characters')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText(TOO_LONG)).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' })).toBeDisabled();
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('a'.repeat(12_000));
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  test('R2.3 R2.5 success posts the text as typed and opens Steps with the plan', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    const parse = await fakeParse(page, 200, OK_BODY);
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast the chicken.\n');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/steps$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Check the steps');
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: 'Roast chicken' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('No issues.')).toBeVisible();
    expect(parse.lastBody()).toEqual({ text: 'Roast the chicken.\n' });
  });

  const MESSAGES: { code: string; status: number; text: string; headers?: Record<string, string> }[] = [
    {
      code: 'bad_request',
      status: 400,
      text: 'The recipe reader could not read this request. Reload the page and try again.',
    },
    { code: 'input_too_long', status: 413, text: TOO_LONG },
    {
      code: 'rate_limited',
      status: 429,
      headers: { 'Retry-After': '120' },
      text: `You have read several recipes in a short time. Try again after 17:02. ${MEANWHILE}`,
    },
    {
      code: 'rate_limited',
      status: 429,
      text: `You have read several recipes in a short time. Try again in a few minutes. ${MEANWHILE}`,
    },
    {
      code: 'daily_cap_reached',
      status: 429,
      headers: { 'Retry-After': '3600' },
      text: 'The recipe reader has reached its limit for today. It starts again at 18:00. Use a sample dinner or open a Dinnerfile instead.',
    },
    {
      code: 'daily_cap_reached',
      status: 429,
      text: 'The recipe reader has reached its limit for today. It starts again tomorrow. Use a sample dinner or open a Dinnerfile instead.',
    },
    {
      code: 'model_unavailable',
      status: 503,
      text: 'The recipe reader is not available on this server right now. Use a sample dinner or open a Dinnerfile instead.',
    },
    {
      code: 'model_refused',
      status: 502,
      text: 'The recipe reader declined to read this text. Check that it is a recipe, or use a sample dinner.',
    },
    {
      code: 'model_output_invalid',
      status: 502,
      text: 'The recipe reader returned steps this app cannot use. Try again, or paste fewer dishes at a time.',
    },
    {
      code: 'internal',
      status: 500,
      text: 'Something went wrong on the server. Try again, or use a sample dinner or a Dinnerfile.',
    },
    {
      code: 'invalid_plan',
      status: 422,
      text: 'Something went wrong on the server. Try again, or use a sample dinner or a Dinnerfile.',
    },
  ];

  for (const message of MESSAGES) {
    const variant = message.headers === undefined ? 'no Retry-After' : 'Retry-After';
    test(`R2.6 ${message.code} (${variant}) shows its copy and keeps the text`, async ({ page }) => {
      await fakeParse(page, message.status, parseError(message.code), message.headers);
      await page.goto('/');
      await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast it.');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' }).click();
      await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toHaveText(message.text);
      await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/menu$/);
      await expect(page.getByLabel('Recipe text')).toHaveValue('Roast it.');
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' })).toBeEnabled();
    });
  }

  test('R2.7 a request that fails without a response shows the network copy', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/parse', (route) => route.abort());
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast it.');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toHaveText(
      'The server could not be reached. Check your connection and try again. Sample dinners you have opened and Dinnerfiles work offline.',
    );
  });

  test('R2.4 a slow request shows the busy copy with seconds; Cancel stops it', async ({ page }) => {
    let release: () => void = () => undefined;
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });
    await page.route('**/api/parse', async (route) => {
      await held;
      await route.abort().catch(() => undefined);
    });
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast it.');
    await page.clock.pauseAt(new Date('2026-11-11T17:00:10Z'));
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' }).click();
    const busy = page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Reading your recipes.' });
    await expect(busy).toContainText('Reading your recipes. This can take up to a minute. 0 s');
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Recipe text')).toBeDisabled();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' })).toBeDisabled();
    await page.clock.runFor(5000);
    await expect(busy).toContainText('Reading your recipes. This can take up to a minute. 5 s');

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText('Stopped reading the recipes.')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(busy).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Recipe text')).toHaveValue('Roast it.');
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' })).toBeEnabled();
    release();
  });

  test('R2.8 the typed text survives a reload', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast the chicken.');
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Recipe text')).toHaveValue('Roast the chicken.');
  });
});

// Paste recipes (T6.3)
export const PASTE_COPY = {
  heading: 'Your own recipes',
  label: 'Recipe text',
  hint: 'Paste the recipes for every dish of one dinner.',
  submit: 'Read recipes',
  cancel: 'Cancel',
  empty: 'Paste at least one recipe.',
  tooLong: 'The recipe text is over 12,000 characters. Shorten it or split it into two dinners.',
  busy: 'Reading your recipes. This can take up to a minute.',
  stopped: 'Stopped reading the recipes.',
} as const;

/** The counter under the recipe text: "9 of 12,000 characters"; `n` is in code points. */
export function recipeCounter(n: number): string {
  return `${n.toLocaleString('en-US')} of 12,000 characters`;
}

/** The elapsed seconds of a running request: "5 s". */
export function elapsedSeconds(seconds: number): string {
  return `${seconds} s`;
}

const MEANWHILE = 'Meanwhile, sample dinners and Dinnerfiles still work.';
const INSTEAD = 'Use a sample dinner or open a Dinnerfile instead.';

/** What happened and what to do next, for a parse that gave no plan (R2.6, R2.7). */
export function parseFailureText(failure: ParseFailure, locale?: string): string {
  switch (failure.kind) {
    case 'bad_request':
      return 'The recipe reader could not read this request. Reload the page and try again.';
    case 'input_too_long':
      return PASTE_COPY.tooLong;
    case 'rate_limited': {
      const when =
        failure.retryAtMs === null
          ? 'Try again in a few minutes.'
          : `Try again after ${formatClock(failure.retryAtMs, locale)}.`;
      return `You have read several recipes in a short time. ${when} ${MEANWHILE}`;
    }
    case 'daily_cap_reached': {
      const when =
        failure.retryAtMs === null ? 'tomorrow' : `at ${formatClock(failure.retryAtMs, locale)}`;
      return `The recipe reader has reached its limit for today. It starts again ${when}. ${INSTEAD}`;
    }
    case 'model_unavailable':
      return `The recipe reader is not available on this server right now. ${INSTEAD}`;
    case 'model_refused':
      return 'The recipe reader declined to read this text. Check that it is a recipe, or use a sample dinner.';
    case 'model_output_invalid':
      return 'The recipe reader returned steps this app cannot use. Try again, or paste fewer dishes at a time.';
    case 'network':
      return 'The server could not be reached. Check your connection and try again. Sample dinners you have opened and Dinnerfiles work offline.';
    case 'internal':
    case 'unexpected':
      return 'Something went wrong on the server. Try again, or use a sample dinner or a Dinnerfile.';
  }
}
'''

let lastRequestId = 0;
/** A fresh id for a parse request, so a late answer to an old request can be ignored. */
export function newRequestId(): number {
  lastRequestId += 1;
  return lastRequestId;
}

'''  // Parse: one request at a time, owned here. The store says `busy`; leaving `busy` aborts it.
  const clock = env.clock ?? systemClock;
  let inFlight: { requestId: number; controller: AbortController } | null = null;
  const syncParse = (): void => {
    const { parse, recipeText } = store.getState();
    if (parse.status !== 'busy') {
      if (inFlight !== null) {
        const stopped = inFlight;
        inFlight = null;
        stopped.controller.abort();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (inFlight?.requestId === parse.requestId) return;
    inFlight?.controller.abort();
    const { requestId } = parse;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    inFlight = { requestId, controller };
    void postParse(env.fetch, recipeText, controller.signal, clock.nowMs()).then((result) => {
      if (inFlight?.requestId === requestId) inFlight = null;
      if (result.ok) {
        store.dispatch({ type: 'parse/done', requestId, plan: result.value, nowMs: clock.nowMs() });
      } else if (result.error.kind === 'cancelled') {
        store.dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text: PASTE_COPY.stopped });
      } else {
        store.dispatch({ type: 'parse/failed', requestId, failure: result.error });
      }
    });
  };

  const unsubscribe = store.subscribe(() => {
    apply();
    fetchSamplesWhenWanted();
    syncParse();
  });
  apply();
  fetchSamplesWhenWanted();
  syncParse();''')
s=s.replace("import { COPY } from '../copy.js';","import { COPY, PASTE_COPY } from '../copy.js';")

cat > src/web/components/RecipePaste.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useId, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { normaliseRecipeText } from '../../core/index.js';
import { elapsedSeconds, PASTE_COPY, parseFailureText, recipeCounter } from '../copy.js';
import { systemClock } from '../state/clock.js';
import { newRequestId, useAppState, useClock, useDispatch, useNow } from '../state/hooks.js';

const MAX_CHARACTERS = 12_000;

/** The running request's seconds; its own component so that only it re-renders each second. */
function Elapsed(props: { startedAtMs: number }): VNode {
  const now = useNow(systemClock, 1000);
  return (
    <span aria-hidden="true"> {elapsedSeconds(Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - props.startedAtMs) / 1000)))}</span>
  );
}

/** Paste recipes, count them against the limit and send them to be read (R2.1 to R2.8). */
export function RecipePaste(): VNode {
  const text = useAppState((state) => state.recipeText);
  const parse = useAppState((state) => state.parse);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();
  const ids = useId();
  const submit = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  const length = [...normaliseRecipeText(text)].length;
  const busy = parse.status === 'busy';
  const reason =
    length === 0 ? PASTE_COPY.empty : length > MAX_CHARACTERS ? PASTE_COPY.tooLong : null;
  const blocked = reason !== null;

  const send = (): void => {
    if (blocked || busy) return;
    dispatch({ type: 'parse/start', requestId: newRequestId(), nowMs: clock.nowMs() });
  };

  const cancel = (): void => {
    dispatch({ type: 'parse/cancel' });
    // The button is enabled again after this render; put focus back where the cook started.
    queueMicrotask(() => submit.current?.focus());
  };

  return (
    <div class="paste">
      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-text`}>{PASTE_COPY.label}</label>
        <p id={`${ids}-hint`} class="muted">
          {PASTE_COPY.hint}
        </p>
        <textarea
          id={`${ids}-text`}
          class="input input--area"
          rows={10}
          maxLength={200_000}
          value={text}
          disabled={busy}
          aria-describedby={`${ids}-hint ${ids}-count`}
          onInput={(event) => dispatch({ type: 'recipe-text/set', text: event.currentTarget.value })}
        />
        <p id={`${ids}-count`} class="muted">
          {recipeCounter(length)}
        </p>
      </div>
      {parse.status === 'failed' && (
        <p role="alert" class="field-error">
          {parseFailureText(parse.failure)}
        </p>
      )}
      <div class="actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button button--primary"
          ref={submit}
          disabled={blocked || busy}
          aria-describedby={blocked ? `${ids}-reason` : undefined}
          onClick={send}
        >
          {PASTE_COPY.submit}
        </button>
        {busy && (
          <button type="button" class="button" onClick={cancel}>
            {PASTE_COPY.cancel}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {blocked && !busy && (
        <p id={`${ids}-reason`} class="muted">
          {reason}
        </p>
      )}
      {parse.status === 'busy' && (
        <p role="status">
          {PASTE_COPY.busy}
          <Elapsed startedAtMs={parse.startedAtMs} />
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* Paste recipes and Dinnerfile */
.paste {
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-2);
}

.input--area {

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && mkdir -p test/web/state && cat > test/web/state/dinnerfile-import.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readDinnerfile } from '../../../src/web/state/dinnerfile-import.js';
import { DEFAULT_KITCHEN } from '../../../src/web/state/store.js';
import { SUNDAY_ROAST, SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE } from '../../support/fixtures.js';

const KITCHEN_NOTICE =
  'The kitchen line in the Dinnerfile is not valid, so the usual kitchen is used: 4 burners, 1 oven, 1 cook.';

function planned(text: string) {
  const result = readDinnerfile(text);
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error('expected the Dinnerfile to be read');
  return result.value;
}

describe('readDinnerfile (R3.2 to R3.5)', () => {
  it('R3.5 SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE opens Plan with the default kitchen and no serve time', () => {
    const load = planned(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE);
    expect(load.route).toBe('plan');
    expect(load.source).toEqual({ kind: 'dinnerfile' });
    expect(load.kitchen).toEqual(DEFAULT_KITCHEN);
    expect(load.serve).toBeNull();
    expect(load.suggested).toBeNull();
    expect(load.notice).toBeNull();
    expect(load.plan.title).toBe('Sunday roast');
    expect(load.plan.steps.map((s) => s.id).sort()).toEqual(
      SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.map((s) => s.id).sort(),
    );
  });

  it('R3.5 a # kitchen line and a # serve line come through', () => {
    const text = `${SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE}# kitchen burners=2 ovens=2 cooks=3\n# serve 19:30\n`;
    const load = planned(text);
    expect(load.kitchen).toEqual({ burners: 2, ovens: 2, cooks: 3 });
    expect(load.serve).toBe('19:30');
    expect(load.notice).toBeNull();
    expect(load.route).toBe('plan');
  });

  it('R3.5 a kitchen line outside the limits gives the default kitchen and the notice', () => {
    const text = `${SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE}# kitchen burners=9 ovens=1 cooks=1\n`;
    const load = planned(text);
    expect(load.kitchen).toEqual({ burners: 4, ovens: 1, cooks: 1 });
    expect(load.notice).toBe(KITCHEN_NOTICE);
  });

  it('R3.5 a plan with an error opens Steps, not Plan', () => {
    // chicken.prep waits for chicken.roast, which waits for chicken.prep: a cycle.
    const text = SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.replace('chicken.prep:\n', 'chicken.prep: chicken.roast\n');
    const load = planned(text);
    expect(load.route).toBe('steps');
    expect(load.plan.steps).toHaveLength(SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.length);
  });

  it('R3.3 a line Make Dinner does not read gives one line problem with the Dinnerfile message', () => {
    const result = readDinnerfile(`${SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE}$(X)\n`);
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: {
        heading: 'This Dinnerfile could not be read. Fix these lines and open it again.',
        problems: [
          {
            kind: 'line',
            line: 57,
            message:
              'Line 57: Make Dinner reads only comments, rules like "chicken.carve: chicken.rest" and recipe lines. Remove this line or rewrite it as one of those.',
          },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  it('R3.3 a file without a # plan line has a problem with no line number', () => {
    const result = readDinnerfile('');
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: {
        heading: 'This Dinnerfile could not be read. Fix these lines and open it again.',
        problems: [
          {
            kind: 'line',
            line: null,
            message:
              'The file has no "# plan" line. Add one near the top, like: # plan "Sunday roast"',
          },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  it('R3.4 minutes=0 is read but invalid: the invalid-plan heading and the issue message', () => {
    const text = SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.replace(
      'minutes=5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=5 title="Carve',
      'minutes=0 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=5 title="Carve',
    );
    expect(readDinnerfile(text)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: {
        heading: 'This Dinnerfile was read, but its plan is not valid:',
        problems: [
          {
            kind: 'issue',
            message: 'Step chicken.carve: minutes must be a whole number from 1 to 480.',
          },
        ],
      },
    });
  });
});
EOF

cat > src/web/state/dinnerfile-import.ts <<'EOF'
import { fromDinnerfile, hasErrors, parseKitchen, validatePlan } from '../../core/index.js';
import type { Result } from '../../core/index.js';
import { err, ok } from '../../core/index.js';
import { DINNERFILE_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { DEFAULT_KITCHEN } from './store.js';
import type { DinnerLoad, ImportProblem } from './store.js';

export const DINNERFILE_FILE_MAX = 1_048_576;

export interface DinnerfileProblems {
  heading: string;
  problems: ImportProblem[];
}

/**
 * Reads a Dinnerfile in the browser, with no request (R3.2). Errors from `fromDinnerfile` are
 * listed in the order returned (R3.3); a plan failing `validatePlan` outright lists its issues
 * (R3.4); otherwise the plan opens Plan, or Steps when it still has errors (R3.5).
 */
export function readDinnerfile(text: string): Result<DinnerLoad, DinnerfileProblems> {
  const read = fromDinnerfile(text);
  if (!read.ok) {
    return err({
      heading: DINNERFILE_COPY.unreadable,
      problems: read.error.map((e) => ({ kind: 'line', line: e.line, message: e.message })),
    });
  }
  const check = validatePlan(read.value.plan);
  if (check.plan === null) {
    return err({
      heading: DINNERFILE_COPY.invalidPlan,
      problems: check.issues.map((issue) => ({ kind: 'issue', message: issue.message })),
    });
  }
  let kitchen = DEFAULT_KITCHEN;
  let notice: string | null = null;
  const stored = read.value.options.kitchen;
  if (stored !== undefined) {
    const parsed = parseKitchen(stored);
    if (parsed.ok) kitchen = parsed.value;
    else notice = DINNERFILE_COPY.kitchenLineIgnored;
  }
  return ok({
    plan: check.plan,
    source: { kind: 'dinnerfile' },
    kitchen,
    serve: read.value.options.serve ?? null,
    suggested: null,
    route: hasErrors(check.issues) ? 'steps' : 'plan',
    notice,
  });
}
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/components/DinnerfilePaste.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useId, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { DINNERFILE_COPY, showLine } from '../copy.js';
import { DINNERFILE_FILE_MAX, readDinnerfile } from '../state/dinnerfile-import.js';
import { useAppState, useClock, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

/** Start and end offsets of the 1-based `line` in `text`, the line break excluded. */
function lineRange(text: string, line: number): [number, number] {
  const lines = text.split('\n');
  const start = lines.slice(0, line - 1).reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.length + 1, 0);
  return [start, start + (lines[line - 1]?.length ?? 0)];
}

/** Paste or open a Dinnerfile and read it in the browser (R3.1 to R3.5). */
export function DinnerfilePaste(): VNode {
  const text = useAppState((state) => state.dinnerfileText);
  const importState = useAppState((state) => state.import);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const clock = useClock();
  const ids = useId();
  const area = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  const empty = text.trim() === '';

  const open = (source: string): void => {
    const result = readDinnerfile(source);
    if (result.ok) dispatch({ type: 'dinner/load', load: result.value, nowMs: clock.nowMs() });
    else dispatch({ type: 'import/failed', ...result.error });
  };

  const choose = async (file: File | undefined): Promise<void> => {
    if (file === undefined) return;
    if (file.size > DINNERFILE_FILE_MAX) {
      dispatch({ type: 'import/failed', heading: DINNERFILE_COPY.fileTooLarge, problems: [] });
      return;
    }
    let content: string;
    try {
      content = await file.text();
    } catch {
      dispatch({ type: 'import/failed', heading: DINNERFILE_COPY.fileUnreadable, problems: [] });
      return;
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'dinnerfile-text/set', text: content });
    open(content);
  };

  const show = (line: number): void => {
    const [start, end] = lineRange(text, line);
    area.current?.focus();
    area.current?.setSelectionRange(start, end);
  };

  return (
    <div class="paste">
      <div class="field">
        <label for={`${ids}-text`}>{DINNERFILE_COPY.label}</label>
        <textarea
          id={`${ids}-text`}
          ref={area}
          class="input input--area input--mono"
          rows={10}
          maxLength={DINNERFILE_FILE_MAX}
          spellcheck={false}
          value={text}
          onInput={(event) =>
            dispatch({ type: 'dinnerfile-text/set', text: event.currentTarget.value })
          }
        />
      </div>
      {importState.status === 'failed' && (
        <div role="alert" class="import-problems">
          <p class="field-error">{importState.heading}</p>
          {importState.problems.length > 0 && (
            <ul>
              {importState.problems.map((problem, n) => (
                <li key={n}>
                  <span>{problem.message}</span>
                  {problem.kind === 'line' && problem.line !== null && (
                    <button type="button" class="button" onClick={() => show(problem.line ?? 1)}>
                      {showLine(problem.line)}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <div class="actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button button--primary"
          disabled={empty}
          aria-describedby={empty ? `${ids}-reason` : undefined}
          onClick={() => open(text)}
        >
          {DINNERFILE_COPY.open}
        </button>
        <label class="button file-button">
          {DINNERFILE_COPY.fileLabel}
          <input
            type="file"
            class="visually-hidden"
            onChange={(event) => {
              const input = event.currentTarget;
              void choose(input.files?.[0]).then(() => {
                input.value = '';
              });
            }}
          />
        </label>
      </div>
      {empty && (
        <p id={`${ids}-reason`} class="muted">
          {DINNERFILE_COPY.empty}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/UnplacedSteps.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import type { Plan } from '../../core/index.js';
import { STEPS_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { StepCard } from './StepCard.js';
import type { IssuePlacement } from './issue-placement.js';

interface Props {
  plan: Plan;
  /** Step indices in screen order. */
  order: readonly number[];
  placement: IssuePlacement;
  editing: number | null;
  readOnly: boolean;
  onEdit: (index: number | null) => void;
}

/**
 * Steps whose `dish` matches no dish of the plan (an imported Dinnerfile can have them).
 * They get a section of their own so that their `unknown_dish` error can be fixed by
 * picking a dish. Nothing is shown when every step has a dish.
 */
export function UnplacedSteps(props: Props): VNode | null {
  const { plan, placement } = props;
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const known = new Set(plan.dishes.map((dish) => dish.id));
  const indices = props.order.filter((index) => {
    const step = plan.steps[index];
    return step !== undefined && !known.has(step.dish);
  });
  if (indices.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section class="dish" aria-labelledby="no-dish-heading">
      <h2 id="no-dish-heading" tabIndex={-1}>
        {STEPS_COPY.noDishHeading}
      </h2>
      <p class="muted">{STEPS_COPY.noDishHint}</p>
      <ol class="card-list step-list">
        {indices.map((index) => {
          const step = plan.steps[index];
          if (step === undefined) return null;
          return (
            <StepCard
              key={index}
              plan={plan}
              step={step}
              index={index}
              issues={placement.byStep.get(index) ?? []}
              editing={props.editing === index}
              readOnly={props.readOnly}
              onEdit={() => props.onEdit(index)}
              onDone={() => props.onEdit(null)}
              onRemove={() => {
                props.onEdit(null);
                dispatch({ type: 'step/remove', index });
                queueMicrotask(() => document.getElementById('issues-heading')?.focus());
              }}
            />
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}

    /** The index and text checks only; scripts/record.ts uses it before any recording exists. */
    export function buildSampleTexts(source: LibrarySource): Result<SampleText[], string[]> {
      const { problems, texts } = analyse(source, false);
      return problems.length > 0 ? err(problems) : ok(texts);
    }
    
    export function buildSampleLibrary(source: LibrarySource): Result<SampleLibrary, string[]> {
      const { problems, loaded } = analyse(source, true);
      if (problems.length > 0) return err(problems);
      const byHash = new Map(loaded.map((sample) => [sample.textSha256, sample]));
      return ok({
        samples: loaded,
        findByText(normalisedText) {
          const sample = byHash.get(sha256Hex(normalisedText));
          return sample !== undefined && sample.text === normalisedText ? sample : undefined;
        },
      });
    }
    
    export function loadSampleLibrary(paths: LibraryPaths): Result<SampleLibrary, string[]> {
      return buildSampleLibrary(readLibrarySource(paths));
    }
    
    export function formatStartupFailure(problems: readonly string[]): string {

import type { Issue, Kitchen, Plan, Result, ServeTime } from '../../core/index.js';
import { err } from '../../core/index.js';

export const SHARE_VERSION = 1;
export const SHARE_URL_MAX = 8_000;
export const SHARE_PAYLOAD_MAX = 65_536;
export const SHARE_INFLATED_MAX = 262_144;

export interface SharedDinner {
  plan: Plan;
  kitchen: Kitchen;
  serve: ServeTime;
}

export type ShareDecodeError =
  | { kind: 'bad_format' }
  | { kind: 'unknown_version'; version: number }
  | { kind: 'too_large' }
  | { kind: 'invalid_plan'; issues: Issue[] };

export function shareBody(_plan: Plan, _kitchen: Kitchen, _serve: ServeTime): unknown[] {
  return [];
}

export function encodeShare(_d: SharedDinner): Promise<Result<string, 'invalid_plan'>> {
  return Promise.resolve(err('invalid_plan'));
}

export function decodeShare(
  _version: number,
  _payload: string,
): Promise<Result<SharedDinner, ShareDecodeError>> {
  return Promise.resolve(err({ kind: 'bad_format' }));
}

export function shareUrl(_base: string, _encoded: string): string {
  return '';
}

cat > test/web/state/share-link.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { deflateRawSync, inflateRawSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { validatePlan } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import type { Plan } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import { loadSampleLibrary } from '../../../src/server/parse/recordings.js';
import {
  decodeShare,
  encodeShare,
  SHARE_URL_MAX,
  shareBody,
  shareUrl,
} from '../../../src/web/state/share-link.js';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../../support/fixtures.js';

/** Two steps in one dish, written out by hand; ids are in canonical order already. */
const TOAST: Plan = {
  title: 'Toast',
  dishes: [{ id: 'toast', name: 'Toast' }],
  steps: [
    {
      id: 'toast.toast',
      dish: 'toast',
      title: 'Toast it',
      minutes: 3,
      appliance: 'oven',
      handsOn: false,
      after: ['toast.slice'],
      holdMin: 5,
      detail: 'Until golden.',
    },
    {
      id: 'toast.slice',
      dish: 'toast',
      title: 'Slice the bread',
      minutes: 2,
      appliance: 'none',
      handsOn: true,
      after: [],
      holdMin: null,
    },
  ],
};
const TOAST_KITCHEN = { burners: 2, ovens: 1, cooks: 1 };

/** The version 1 body of TOAST, worked out by hand from the design (steps sorted by id). */
const TOAST_BODY: unknown[] = [
  'Toast',
  [['toast', 'Toast']],
  [
    ['toast.slice', 0, 'Slice the bread', 2, 2, 1, [], null],
    ['toast.toast', 0, 'Toast it', 3, 1, 0, [0], 5, 'Until golden.'],
  ],
  [2, 1, 1],
  '08:15',
];

function base64url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  return Buffer.from(bytes).toString('base64url');
}

/** A payload as the design writes it: JSON, UTF-8, raw deflate, base64url. */
function payloadOf(body: unknown): string {
  return base64url(deflateRawSync(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body), 'utf8')));
}

async function decodeBody(body: unknown) {
  return decodeShare(1, payloadOf(body));
}

describe('shareBody and encodeShare (R8.3)', () => {
  it('R8.3 the version 1 body of a two-step plan is the hand-written array', () => {
    expect(shareBody(TOAST, TOAST_KITCHEN, '08:15')).toEqual(TOAST_BODY);
  });

  it('R8.3 the payload inflates to that body, behind "1."', async () => {
    const encoded = await encodeShare({ plan: TOAST, kitchen: TOAST_KITCHEN, serve: '08:15' });
    expect(encoded.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!encoded.ok) return;
    expect(encoded.value.startsWith('1.')).toBe(true);
    const json = inflateRawSync(Buffer.from(encoded.value.slice(2), 'base64url')).toString('utf8');
    expect(JSON.parse(json)).toEqual(TOAST_BODY);
  });

  it('R8.4 a plan with an error has no link: invalid_plan', async () => {
    const broken: Plan = {
      ...TOAST,
      steps: TOAST.steps.map((s) => (s.id === 'toast.toast' ? { ...s, after: ['toast.slise'] } : s)),
    };
    expect(await encodeShare({ plan: broken, kitchen: TOAST_KITCHEN, serve: '08:15' })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: 'invalid_plan',
    });
  });

  it('R8.9 decodeShare gives back the canonical plan, the kitchen and the serving time', async () => {
    const encoded = await encodeShare({ plan: TOAST, kitchen: TOAST_KITCHEN, serve: '08:15' });
    if (!encoded.ok) throw new Error('expected a link');
    const decoded = await decodeShare(1, encoded.value.slice(2));
    expect(decoded).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: {
        plan: { ...TOAST, steps: [TOAST.steps[1], TOAST.steps[0]] },
        kitchen: TOAST_KITCHEN,
        serve: '08:15',
      },
    });
  });
});

describe('decodeShare errors (R8.5, R8.6)', () => {
  it('R8.6 version 2 is unknown_version 2', async () => {
    expect(await decodeShare(2, payloadOf(TOAST_BODY))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'unknown_version', version: 2 },
    });
  });

  it('R8.6 a "*" character is bad_format', async () => {
    expect(await decodeShare(1, 'abc*def')).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 base64url of the wrong length is bad_format', async () => {
    expect(await decodeShare(1, 'A')).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 bytes that are not deflate are bad_format', async () => {
    expect(await decodeShare(1, base64url(Buffer.from('not deflate at all')))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'bad_format' },
    });
  });

  it('R8.6 deflate of 300 KB of zeros is too_large (inflated limit)', async () => {
    const bomb = base64url(deflateRawSync(Buffer.alloc(300_000)));
    expect(bomb.length).toBeLessThan(65_536);
    expect(await decodeShare(1, bomb)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'too_large' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 a payload over 65,536 characters is too_large', async () => {
    expect(await decodeShare(1, 'A'.repeat(65_537))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'too_large' },
    });
  });

  it('R8.6 JSON that is not JSON is bad_format', async () => {
    const payload = base64url(deflateRawSync(Buffer.from('{nope', 'utf8')));
    expect(await decodeShare(1, payload)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 a body of the wrong shape is bad_format', async () => {
    expect(await decodeBody({ title: 'Toast' })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'bad_format' },
    });
  });

  it('R8.6 an after index out of range is bad_format', async () => {
    const body = structuredClone(TOAST_BODY) as unknown[][][];
    (body[2]?.[1] as unknown[])[6] = [5];
    expect(await decodeBody(body)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 a dish index out of range is bad_format', async () => {
    const body = structuredClone(TOAST_BODY) as unknown[][][];
    (body[2]?.[0] as unknown[])[1] = 3;
    expect(await decodeBody(body)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 a bad serving time is bad_format', async () => {
    const body = [...TOAST_BODY.slice(0, 4), '25:00'];
    expect(await decodeBody(body)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 a kitchen outside the limits is bad_format', async () => {
    const body = [...TOAST_BODY.slice(0, 3), [9, 1, 1], '08:15'];
    expect(await decodeBody(body)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: { kind: 'bad_format' } });
  });

  it('R8.6 a cycle is invalid_plan with the cycle issue', async () => {
    const body = structuredClone(TOAST_BODY) as unknown[][][];
    (body[2]?.[0] as unknown[])[6] = [1];
    const result = await decodeBody(body);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok || result.error.kind !== 'invalid_plan') throw new Error('expected invalid_plan');
    expect(result.error.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toContain('cycle');
  });
});

describe('shareUrl (R8.3)', () => {
  it('R8.3 puts the encoded payload after #/share/', () => {
    expect(shareUrl('http://127.0.0.1:65535/', '1.abc')).toBe('http://127.0.0.1:65535/#/share/1.abc');
  });
});

describe('every recorded sample fits in a link (R8.4)', () => {
  const library = loadSampleLibrary({ samplesDir: 'samples', recordingsDir: 'recordings' });

  it('R8.4 the library loads', () => {
    expect(library.ok).toBe(true);
  });

  const lengths: Record<string, number> = {};
  it('R8.4 each sample link is under SHARE_URL_MAX with base http://127.0.0.1:65535/', async () => {
    if (!library.ok) throw new Error('library did not load');
    expect(library.value.samples).toHaveLength(5);
    for (const sample of library.value.samples) {
      expect(validatePlan(sample.plan).plan).not.toBeNull();
      const encoded = await encodeShare({
        plan: sample.plan,
        kitchen: sample.kitchen,
        serve: sample.servingTime,
      });
      if (!encoded.ok) throw new Error(`${sample.id} has no link`);
      lengths[sample.id] = shareUrl('http://127.0.0.1:65535/', encoded.value).length;
      expect(lengths[sample.id]).toBeLessThan(SHARE_URL_MAX);
    }
    expect(Object.keys(lengths).sort()).toEqual([
      'halloween-party',
      'korean-table',
      'sunday-roast',
      'vegetarian-feast',
      'weeknight-curry',
    ]);
  });

  it('R8.4 the H1 plan with the home kitchen also fits', async () => {
    const encoded = await encodeShare({ plan: SUNDAY_ROAST, kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN, serve: '19:30' });
    expect(encoded.ok).toBe(true);
    if (encoded.ok) {
      expect(shareUrl('http://127.0.0.1:65535/', encoded.value).length).toBeLessThan(SHARE_URL_MAX);
    }
  });
});
EOF
