[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/state/share-link.ts <<'EOF'
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  canonicalPlan,
  err,
  hasErrors,
  LIMITS,
  ok,
  parseKitchen,
  ServeTimeSchema,
  validatePlan,
} from '../../core/index.js';
import type { Appliance, Issue, Kitchen, Plan, Result, ServeTime } from '../../core/index.js';

export const SHARE_VERSION = 1;
/** RFC 9110 section 4.1 recommends supporting URIs of 8,000 octets. */
export const SHARE_URL_MAX = 8_000;
/** Accepted on load: payload characters, and inflated bytes (a deflate bomb stops here). */
export const SHARE_PAYLOAD_MAX = 65_536;
export const SHARE_INFLATED_MAX = 262_144;

export interface SharedDinner {
  plan: Plan;
  kitchen: Kitchen;
  serve: ServeTime;
}

export type ShareDecodeError =
  | { kind: 'bad_format' }
  | { kind: 'unknown_version'; version: number }
  | { kind: 'too_large' }
  | { kind: 'invalid_plan'; issues: Issue[] };

const APPLIANCES: readonly Appliance[] = ['burner', 'oven', 'none'];

/**
 * The version 1 body (plan-ui design, "Share link"), built from the canonical plan:
 * `[title, [[dishId, name]], [[stepId, dishIndex, title, minutes, appliance, handsOn,
 * [afterIndex], holdMin, detail?]], [burners, ovens, cooks], "HH:MM"]`.
 * Exported for tests; the plan must have no `after` entry without a step.
 */
export function shareBody(plan: Plan, kitchen: Kitchen, serve: ServeTime): unknown[] {
  const canonical = canonicalPlan(plan);
  const dishIndex = new Map(canonical.dishes.map((d, i) => [d.id, i]));
  const stepIndex = new Map(canonical.steps.map((s, i) => [s.id, i]));
  return [
    canonical.title,
    canonical.dishes.map((d) => [d.id, d.name]),
    canonical.steps.map((s) => {
      const row: unknown[] = [
        s.id,
        dishIndex.get(s.dish) ?? -1,
        s.title,
        s.minutes,
        APPLIANCES.indexOf(s.appliance),
        s.handsOn ? 1 : 0,
        s.after.map((id) => stepIndex.get(id) ?? -1),
        s.holdMin,
      ];
      if (s.detail !== undefined) row.push(s.detail);
      return row;
    }),
    [kitchen.burners, kitchen.ovens, kitchen.cooks],
    serve,
  ];
}

async function collect(stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>, limit: number): Promise<Uint8Array | null> {
  const reader = stream.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let total = 0;
  for (;;) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    total += value.length;
    if (total > limit) {
      await reader.cancel();
      return null;
    }
    chunks.push(value);
  }
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(total);
  let offset = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    bytes.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.length;
  }
  return bytes;
}

async function deflate(bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const stream = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new CompressionStream('deflate-raw'));
  return (await collect(stream, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)) ?? new Uint8Array();
}

/** Null when the bytes are not deflate or inflate past `limit`; `'large'` past the limit. */
async function inflate(bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, limit: number): Promise<Uint8Array | 'large' | null> {
  try {
    const stream = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw'));
    return (await collect(stream, limit)) ?? 'large';
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function toBase64Url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
  }
  return btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
}

function fromBase64Url(text: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null {
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(text) || text.length % 4 === 1) return null;
  try {
    const binary = atob(text.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'));
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i += 1) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    return bytes;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** `"1.<base64url>"`, or `invalid_plan` when the plan has an error (no index for a missing step). */
export async function encodeShare(d: SharedDinner): Promise<Result<string, 'invalid_plan'>> {
  const check = validatePlan(d.plan);
  if (check.plan === null || hasErrors(check.issues)) return err('invalid_plan');
  const json = JSON.stringify(shareBody(check.plan, d.kitchen, d.serve));
  const compressed = await deflate(new TextEncoder().encode(json));
  return ok(`${SHARE_VERSION}.${toBase64Url(compressed)}`);
}

const StepRowSchema = z.tuple(
  [
    z.string(),
    z.int().min(0).max(LIMITS.dishesMax),
    z.string(),
    z.number(),
    z.union([z.literal(0), z.literal(1), z.literal(2)]),
    z.union([z.literal(0), z.literal(1)]),
    z.array(z.int().min(0)).max(LIMITS.afterMax),
    z.number().nullable(),
  ],
  z.string(),
);
const BodySchema = z.tuple([
  z.string(),
  z.array(z.tuple([z.string(), z.string()])).max(LIMITS.dishesMax),
  z.array(StepRowSchema).max(LIMITS.stepsMax),
  z.tuple([z.number(), z.number(), z.number()]),
  z.string(),
]);

/** Never throws or rejects: every failure is a `ShareDecodeError`. */
export async function decodeShare(
  version: number,
  payload: string,
): Promise<Result<SharedDinner, ShareDecodeError>> {
  const bad = err<ShareDecodeError>({ kind: 'bad_format' });
  if (version !== SHARE_VERSION) return err({ kind: 'unknown_version', version });
  if (payload.length > SHARE_PAYLOAD_MAX) return err({ kind: 'too_large' });
  const compressed = fromBase64Url(payload);
  if (compressed === null) return bad;
  const inflated = await inflate(compressed, SHARE_INFLATED_MAX);
  if (inflated === 'large') return err({ kind: 'too_large' });
  if (inflated === null) return bad;

  let json: unknown;
  try {
    json = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(inflated));
  } catch {
    return bad;
  }
  const body = BodySchema.safeParse(json);
  if (!body.success) return bad;
  const [title, dishRows, stepRows, kitchenRow, serve] = body.data;

  const rawSteps = [];
  for (const [id, dishAt, stepTitle, minutes, appliance, handsOn, afterAt, holdMin, detail] of stepRows) {
    const dish = dishRows[dishAt];
    const after = afterAt.map((at) => stepRows[at]?.[0]);
    const appliancePicked = APPLIANCES[appliance];
    if (dish === undefined || after.includes(undefined) || appliancePicked === undefined) return bad;
    rawSteps.push({
      id,
      dish: dish[0],
      title: stepTitle,
      minutes,
      appliance: appliancePicked,
      handsOn: handsOn === 1,
      after: after as string[],
      holdMin,
      ...(detail === undefined ? {} : { detail }),
    });
  }
  const check = validatePlan({
    title,
    dishes: dishRows.map(([id, name]) => ({ id, name })),
    steps: rawSteps,
  });
  if (check.plan === null || hasErrors(check.issues)) {
    return err({ kind: 'invalid_plan', issues: check.issues });
  }
  const kitchen = parseKitchen({
    burners: kitchenRow[0],
    ovens: kitchenRow[1],
    cooks: kitchenRow[2],
  });
  const time = ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(serve);
  if (!kitchen.ok || !time.success) return bad;
  return ok({ plan: canonicalPlan(check.plan), kitchen: kitchen.value, serve: time.data });
}

/** `base` is origin plus pathname; the payload goes in the fragment, which browsers never send. */
export function shareUrl(base: string, encoded: string): string {
  return `${base}#/share/${encoded}`;
}
EOF

import fc from 'fast-check';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { canonicalPlan, hasErrors, validatePlan } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import { decodeShare, encodeShare } from '../../../src/web/state/share-link.js';
import { arbPlanAndKitchen, arbServeTime } from '../../support/arbitraries.js';
import { fcParams } from '../../support/fc-params.js';

const KINDS = ['bad_format', 'unknown_version', 'too_large', 'invalid_plan'];
const BASE64URL = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_';

/** Mostly small plans; now and then one that reaches the limits of 12 dishes and 80 steps. */
const arbPair = fc.oneof(
  { weight: 9, arbitrary: arbPlanAndKitchen() },
  { weight: 1, arbitrary: arbPlanAndKitchen({ large: true }) },
);

describe('share-link properties', () => {
  it('P-UI1 round trip: decodeShare(encodeShare(d)) is the canonical plan, kitchen and serving time', async () => {
    await fc.assert(
      fc.asyncProperty(arbPair, arbServeTime(), async ({ plan, kitchen }, serve) => {
        const encoded = await encodeShare({ plan, kitchen, serve });
        expect(encoded.ok).toBe(true);
        if (!encoded.ok) return;
        expect(encoded.value.startsWith('1.')).toBe(true);
        const decoded = await decodeShare(1, encoded.value.slice(2));
        expect(decoded).toEqual({
          ok: true,
          value: { plan: canonicalPlan(plan), kitchen, serve },
        });
      }),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });

  /** Total, and sound when it says yes: a plan that comes out has no error issue. */
  async function expectTotal(version: number, payload: string): Promise<void> {
    const result = await decodeShare(version, payload);
    if (result.ok) {
      const check = validatePlan(result.value.plan);
      expect(check.plan).not.toBeNull();
      expect(hasErrors(check.issues)).toBe(false);
    } else {
      expect(KINDS).toContain(result.error.kind);
    }
  }

  it('P-UI2 decode is total: any string, any version', async () => {
    await fc.assert(
      fc.asyncProperty(fc.integer({ min: -2, max: 999 }), fc.string({ unit: 'binary' }), expectTotal),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });

  it('P-UI2 decode is total: strings of base64url characters (past the character check)', async () => {
    const arbText = fc
      .array(fc.constantFrom(...BASE64URL), { maxLength: 300 })
      .map((chars) => chars.join(''));
    await fc.assert(fc.asyncProperty(arbText, (text) => expectTotal(1, text)), fcParams());
  });

  it('P-UI2 decode is total: a real payload with one character replaced, removed or added', async () => {
    await fc.assert(
      fc.asyncProperty(
        arbPair,
        arbServeTime(),
        fc.double({ min: 0, max: 1, noNaN: true }),
        fc.constantFrom(...BASE64URL, '*', ' ', '\u0000'),
        fc.constantFrom('replace', 'remove', 'insert'),
        async ({ plan, kitchen }, serve, where, char, how) => {
          const encoded = await encodeShare({ plan, kitchen, serve });
          if (!encoded.ok) throw new Error('the generator gave an invalid plan');
          const payload = encoded.value.slice(2);
          const at = Math.min(payload.length - 1, Math.floor(where * payload.length));
          const mutated =
            how === 'replace'
              ? payload.slice(0, at) + char + payload.slice(at + 1)
              : how === 'remove'
                ? payload.slice(0, at) + payload.slice(at + 1)
                : payload.slice(0, at) + char + payload.slice(at);
          await expectTotal(1, mutated);
        },
      ),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > e2e/share.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { encodeShare } from '../src/web/state/share-link.js';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import { FIXED_NOW, seedDinner } from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

const STORED = { ...SUNDAY_ROAST, title: 'My stored dinner' };

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
});

/** The payload of a link to SUNDAY_ROAST in the home kitchen, served at 19:30. */
async function sundayRoastPayload(): Promise<string> {
  const encoded = await encodeShare({ plan: SUNDAY_ROAST, kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN, serve: '19:30' });
  if (!encoded.ok) throw new Error('expected a link');
  return encoded.value;
}

async function listText(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  return page.locator('#plan-list').innerText();
}

test.describe('opening a share link', () => {
  test('R8.5 a link opens Plan in a new context with the same list as the source page', async ({
    page,
    browser,
  }) => {
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page.getByText('Start at 17:40')).toBeVisible();
    const source = await listText(page);

    const context = await browser.newContext({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });
    const other = await context.newPage();
    await other.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
    await other.goto(`/#/share/${await sundayRoastPayload()}`);
    await expect(other).toHaveURL(/#\/plan$/);
    await expect(other.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Sunday roast');
    await expect(other.getByText('Start at 17:40')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(other.getByText('Opened a shared dinner.')).toBeVisible();
    expect(await listText(other)).toBe(source);
    await context.close();
  });

  test('R8.6 a damaged link opens Menu with the damaged-link copy and keeps the stored dinner', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await seedDinner(page, STORED, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    const payload = await sundayRoastPayload();
    const damaged = `${payload.slice(0, 12)}${payload[12] === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A'}${payload.slice(13)}`;
    await page.goto(`/#/share/${damaged}`);
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/menu$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Pick a dinner');
    await expect(
      page.getByText(
        'This share link is damaged or incomplete, so it could not be opened. Ask for the link again, or open a Dinnerfile.',
      ),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await page
      .getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dinner' })
      .getByRole('link', { name: 'Plan' })
      .click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('My stored dinner');
  });

  test('R8.6 a version 2 link shows the version copy', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/#/share/2.abc');
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/menu$/);
    await expect(
      page.getByText(
        'This share link was made by a newer version of Make Dinner and cannot be opened here.',
      ),
    ).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('R8.6 a link while a dinner is stored asks before replacing', async ({ page }) => {

  const target = parseHash(win.location.hash);
  if (target.kind === 'route') {
    store.dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: target.route, cookRegistered: env.cookRegistered });
  }
  const replaceHash = (): void => {
    const desired = hashOf(store.getState().route);
    if (win.location.hash !== desired) win.history.replaceState(null, '', desired);
  };

  // A share link is decoded in the browser (R8.5). The fragment is replaced before the state
  // changes, so the app's own hash sync adds no history entry and the payload leaves the bar.
  const clock = env.clock ?? systemClock;
  const openShare = async (version: number, payload: string): Promise<void> => {
    const decoded = await decodeShare(version, payload);
    if (!decoded.ok) {
      const { error } = decoded;
      const text =
        error.kind === 'unknown_version'
          ? SHARE_COPY.version
          : error.kind === 'invalid_plan'
            ? SHARE_COPY.invalidPlan
            : SHARE_COPY.damaged;
      win.history.replaceState(null, '', hashOf('menu'));
      store.dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: 'menu', cookRegistered: env.cookRegistered });
      store.dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text });
      return;
    }
    // With a dinner stored the cook is asked on the Menu; without one the shared plan opens.
    const asks = store.getState().dinner !== null;
    win.history.replaceState(null, '', hashOf(asks ? 'menu' : 'plan'));
    if (asks) {
      store.dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: 'menu', cookRegistered: env.cookRegistered });
    }
    store.dispatch({
      type: 'dinner/load',
      nowMs: clock.nowMs(),
      load: {
        plan: decoded.value.plan,
        source: { kind: 'share' },
        kitchen: decoded.value.kitchen,
        serve: decoded.value.serve,
        suggested: null,
        route: 'plan',
        notice: SHARE_COPY.opened,
      },
    });
  };
  if (target.kind === 'share') void openShare(target.version, target.payload);
  else replaceHash();

  const onHashChange = (): void => {
    const next = parseHash(win.location.hash);
    if (next.kind === 'share') {
      void openShare(next.version, next.payload);
      return;
    }
    if (next.kind === 'route' && next.route !== store.getState().route) {
      store.dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: next.route, cookRegistered: env.cookRegistered });
    }
    replaceHash();
  };
'''

cat > src/web/state/clipboard.ts <<'EOF'
/** Writes text to the clipboard; false when the browser refuses or has no clipboard (R8.7). */
export async function copyText(
  clipboard: Pick<Clipboard, 'writeText'> | undefined,
  text: string,
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (clipboard === undefined) return false;
  try {
    await clipboard.writeText(text);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** Offers `text` as a download named `filename` (R8.2). */
export function downloadText(doc: Document, filename: string, text: string): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }));
  const link = doc.createElement('a');
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  doc.body.append(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 0);
}

/** Selects everything inside `element`, so the cook can copy by hand. */
export function selectContents(doc: Document, element: Element): void {
  const selection = doc.defaultView?.getSelection();
  if (selection === null || selection === undefined) return;
  selection.removeAllRanges();
  const range = doc.createRange();
  range.selectNodeContents(element);
  selection.addRange(range);
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/DinnerfilePanel.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useId, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { EXPORT_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { copyText, downloadText, selectContents } from '../state/clipboard.js';
import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

/** The Dinnerfile as text with Copy and Download, and the line on running it with Make (R8.2). */
export function DinnerfilePanel(props: { text: string }): VNode {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const headingId = useId();
  const pre = useRef<HTMLPreElement>(null);

  const copy = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (await copyText(navigator.clipboard, props.text)) {
      dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text: EXPORT_COPY.copiedDinnerfile });
    } else {
      if (pre.current !== null) selectContents(document, pre.current);
      dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text: EXPORT_COPY.copyFailed });
    }
  };

  return (
    <section class="panel" aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <h3 id={headingId}>{EXPORT_COPY.dinnerfile}</h3>
      <pre ref={pre} class="dinnerfile-text mono" tabIndex={0}>
        {props.text}
      </pre>
      <div class="actions">
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={() => void copy()}>
          {EXPORT_COPY.copy}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          onClick={() => downloadText(document, 'Dinnerfile', props.text)}
        >
          {EXPORT_COPY.download}
        </button>
      </div>
      <p class="muted">{EXPORT_COPY.makeLine}</p>
    </section>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/ExportBar.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { EXPORT_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { DinnerfilePanel } from './DinnerfilePanel.js';

type Panel = 'dinnerfile' | null;

/** Dinnerfile, and Print (R8.1); a button opens its panel below, tapping it again closes it. */
export function ExportBar(props: { dinnerfileText: string }): VNode {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<Panel>(null);
  const toggle = (panel: Exclude<Panel, null>): void => setOpen(open === panel ? null : panel);
  return (
    <div class="export">
      <div class="actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          aria-expanded={open === 'dinnerfile'}
          onClick={() => toggle('dinnerfile')}
        >
          {EXPORT_COPY.dinnerfile}
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={() => window.print()}>
          {EXPORT_COPY.print}
        </button>
      </div>
      {open === 'dinnerfile' && <DinnerfilePanel text={props.dinnerfileText} />}
    </div>
  );
}

/** 80 steps in 12 dishes with 1,000 characters of detail each: too long for a link. */
function hugePlan(): Plan {
  let seed = 12345;
  const noise = (n: number): string => {
    let out = '';
    while (out.length < n) {
      seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648;
      out += seed.toString(36);
    }
    return out.slice(0, n);
  };
  const dishes = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({ id: `dish-${i + 1}`, name: `Dish ${i + 1}` }));
  const steps = Array.from({ length: 80 }, (_, i) => ({
    id: `dish-${(i % 12) + 1}.step-${i + 1}`,
    dish: `dish-${(i % 12) + 1}`,
    title: `Step ${i + 1}`,
    minutes: 1,
    appliance: 'none' as const,
    handsOn: false,
    after: [],
    holdMin: null,
    detail: noise(1000),
  }));
  return { title: 'Huge dinner', dishes, steps };
}

cat > src/web/components/SharePanel.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { EXPORT_COPY, shareTooLong } from '../copy.js';

/** The link for the current dinner, computed by an effect when the dinner changes (R8.3, R8.4). */
export type ShareState =
  | { status: 'pending' }
  | { status: 'ready'; url: string }
  | { status: 'too_long'; length: number }
  | { status: 'unavailable' };

interface Props {
  share: ShareState;
  onCopy: () => void;
  /** Changes when a copy failed: the link is selected so it can be copied by hand (R8.7). */
  selectToken: number;
}

export function SharePanel(props: Props): VNode {
  const headingId = useId();
  const fieldId = useId();
  const field = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (props.selectToken > 0) field.current?.select();
  }, [props.selectToken]);

  const { share } = props;
  return (
    <section class="panel" aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <h3 id={headingId}>{EXPORT_COPY.shareLink}</h3>
      {share.status === 'too_long' && <p>{shareTooLong(share.length)}</p>}
      {share.status === 'ready' && (
        <>
          <div class="field">
            <label for={fieldId}>{EXPORT_COPY.linkLabel}</label>
            <input
              id={fieldId}
              ref={field}
              class="input input--mono"
              type="text"
              readOnly
              value={share.url}
            />
          </div>
          <div class="actions">
            <button type="button" class="button" onClick={props.onCopy}>
              {EXPORT_COPY.copyLink}
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/ExportBar.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { Kitchen, Plan, ServeTime } from '../../core/index.js';
import { EXPORT_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { copyText } from '../state/clipboard.js';
import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { encodeShare, SHARE_URL_MAX, shareUrl } from '../state/share-link.js';
import { DinnerfilePanel } from './DinnerfilePanel.js';
import { SharePanel } from './SharePanel.js';
import type { ShareState } from './SharePanel.js';

type Panel = 'dinnerfile' | 'share' | null;

interface Props {
  dinnerfileText: string;
  plan: Plan;
  kitchen: Kitchen;
  serve: ServeTime;
}

/** Dinnerfile, Share link and Print (R8.1); a button opens its panel, tapping it again closes it. */
export function ExportBar(props: Props): VNode {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<Panel>(null);
  const [share, setShare] = useState<ShareState>({ status: 'pending' });
  const [selectToken, setSelectToken] = useState(0);
  const latest = useRef(share);
  latest.current = share;

  // The link is ready before the tap, so the copy happens inside the user's gesture (Safari).
  useEffect(() => {
    let stale = false;
    setShare({ status: 'pending' });
    void encodeShare({ plan: props.plan, kitchen: props.kitchen, serve: props.serve }).then(
      (encoded) => {
        if (stale) return;
        if (!encoded.ok) return setShare({ status: 'unavailable' });
        const url = shareUrl(`${location.origin}${location.pathname}`, encoded.value);
        setShare(
          url.length > SHARE_URL_MAX
            ? { status: 'too_long', length: url.length }
            : { status: 'ready', url },
        );
      },
    );
    return () => {
      stale = true;
    };
  }, [props.plan, props.kitchen, props.serve]);

  const copyLink = async (): Promise<void> => {
    const current = latest.current;
    if (current.status !== 'ready') return;
    if (await copyText(navigator.clipboard, current.url)) {
      dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text: EXPORT_COPY.copiedLink });
    } else {
      setSelectToken((token) => token + 1);
      dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text: EXPORT_COPY.copyFailed });
    }
  };

  const toggle = (panel: Exclude<Panel, null>): void => {
    const opening = open !== panel;
    setOpen(opening ? panel : null);
    if (opening && panel === 'share') void copyLink();
  };

  return (
    <div class="export">
      <div class="actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          aria-expanded={open === 'dinnerfile'}
          onClick={() => toggle('dinnerfile')}
        >
          {EXPORT_COPY.dinnerfile}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          aria-expanded={open === 'share'}
          onClick={() => toggle('share')}
        >
          {EXPORT_COPY.shareLink}
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={() => window.print()}>
          {EXPORT_COPY.print}
        </button>
      </div>
      {open === 'dinnerfile' && <DinnerfilePanel text={props.dinnerfileText} />}
      {open === 'share' && (
        <SharePanel share={share} onCopy={() => void copyLink()} selectToken={selectToken} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF

test.describe('print layout (R8.8)', () => {
  for (const scheme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
    test(`R8.8 print shows only the title, summary, plan list and Dinnerfile, black on white (${scheme})`, async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      await page.goto('/');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Dinnerfile', exact: true }).click();
      await expect(page.locator('main pre')).toHaveCount(2);
      // On screen the print-only Dinnerfile is not shown.
      await expect(page.locator('pre.print-only')).toBeHidden();

      await page.emulateMedia({ media: 'print', colorScheme: scheme });

      // Exactly these four blocks of the screen are visible, and nothing outside the screen.
      const shown = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const visible = (parent: Element | null): string[] =>
          [...(parent?.children ?? [])]
            .filter((el) => getComputedStyle(el).display !== 'none')
            .map((el) => el.tagName + (el.id === '' ? '' : `#${el.id}`));
        return {
          app: visible(document.querySelector('.app')),
          main: visible(document.querySelector('main')),
        };
      });
      expect(shown).toEqual({ app: ['MAIN'], main: ['H1', 'DIV', 'SECTION#plan-list', 'PRE'] });
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Sunday roast');
      await expect(page.locator('.summary')).toContainText('Start at 17:40');
      await expect(page.locator('.summary')).toContainText('Serve at 19:30');
      expect(await page.locator('pre.print-only').textContent()).toBe(EXPORTED);
      await expect(page.locator('header')).toBeHidden();
      await expect(page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dinner' })).toBeHidden();
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Kitchen' })).toBeHidden();
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Timeline' })).toBeHidden();
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Take the plan with you' })).toBeHidden();
      await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Dinnerfile' })).toBeHidden();

      // Black on white for every visible element, in both colour schemes.
      const colours = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const bad: string[] = [];
        for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('main h1, main .summary, main .summary *, #plan-list, #plan-list *, pre.print-only')) {
          const style = getComputedStyle(el);
          if (style.color !== 'rgb(0, 0, 0)') bad.push(`${el.tagName} color ${style.color}`);
          if (style.backgroundColor !== 'rgb(255, 255, 255)') {
            bad.push(`${el.tagName} background ${style.backgroundColor}`);
          }
        }
        return bad;
      });
      expect(colours).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
});

T6 (paste recipes and import a Dinnerfile) and T7 (export, share and print) are done and ticked in `.kiro/specs/plan-ui/tasks.md`. T8 is untouched. The full suite passes: 190 e2e tests on both projects, plus unit, properties, typecheck, lint, format, contrast and build.

## Decisions I made
- **Download type:** `text/x-makefile` instead of `text/plain`. Chromium renames a `text/plain` download to `Dinnerfile.txt`, which breaks `make -f Dinnerfile`. R8.2 asks for `text/plain`, so this departs from its wording. It keeps the file name and still opens as plain text.
- **Share link lengths:** Real sample links are 1,015 to 1,639 characters, against the design's estimate of about 2,800. They're recorded in a comment in `share-link.test.ts`.
- **Replace prompt:** It now sits at the top of the Menu, not inside the samples section, because a pasted recipe can trigger it too.
- **Cancelling a parse:** It sets the notice "Stopped reading the recipes." and moves focus back to "Read recipes".
- **Elapsed seconds:** The elapsed seconds in the busy line are `aria-hidden`, so a screen reader doesn't announce every second.
- **Steps with no dish:** The new section (`UnplacedSteps.tsx`) appears when a step's dish matches no dish. Its step editor's Dish select shows a disabled "(not a dish in this plan)" option so the current value is honest. It is tested through a Dinnerfile import in `dinnerfile.spec.ts`.
- **Share button timing:** The "Share link" button was added in T7.5, together with its panel, not in T7.4. The bar had no dead button in between.

## Screenshots (390×844, light and dark)
I looked at the Menu with a rate-limit error and a Dinnerfile problem list showing, and at the Plan screen with the Dinnerfile panel and the share panel open. Type scale and rhythm are as `ui.md` asks, every visible target is at least 48 px (only the visually hidden file input is smaller), and the page doesn't scroll sideways (390/390). I made one change: "Copy link" sat about 4 px under the link field, so I added `.panel .field { margin-bottom }`. I did not re-shoot after that change.

## Not verified
- **Safari clipboard:** I tested the copy-inside-the-gesture design only in Chromium.
- **Print output:** It is checked through `emulateMedia`, not an actual print preview.
- **Mutation check:** I ran it only on P-UI1 (dropping `detail` from `shareBody` made it fail).
- **Error boundary:** R10.2 is still untested, as decided in T1.6.

## Open points
- **Duplicated schemas:** `src/web/state/api.ts` still defines `SampleItemSchema` and `ParseResponseSchema`. `src/core/index.ts` now exports its own, so the duplicates could move to core. I left them alone, since that is outside these tasks.
- **Over-long share links:** A share fragment over 65,536 characters doesn't match the route pattern, so it is treated as an unknown hash and falls back to the stored route. It shows no "too large" message. The T1.5 route tests fixed that pattern, so I left it.

Working rule: when the design is ambiguous or slightly off in a way that touches no requirement's intent, choose the reading closest to the requirement and to ui.md, write it under "Decisions I made", and continue. Stop and ask only when a requirement would have to change.

Do not commit. Do not start `npm run dev`. End with the report kiro-conduct.md asks for, with the real counts from `npm test`, `npm run test:props`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npx prettier --check .`, `npm run build` and `npm run test:e2e`.

Four reviewed changes come first (I looked at the Plan screen in headless Chromium at phone and desktop widths). Do them test first, before T8, and update the plan-ui requirements, design copy table and steering wording (product.md glossary "Critical", ui.md) in the same change so text and code agree; you are allowed to edit those two steering files for this wording only.

A. "Critical" is on 14 of 17 steps of the Sunday roast, so it tells the cook nothing: a just-in-time schedule gives every hot step slack 0. Replace the display rule with the critical path. Add a pure core function `criticalPath(schedule): string[]` (src/core, exported from index.ts, works for Schedule and RebuiltSchedule): start from the step that ends at the serving minute (if several, the one with the latest start... state a deterministic tie-break: longest minutes, then id), and walk backwards: the binder of step s is a step that ends exactly when s starts and is either a prerequisite of s (preferred) or holds a lane s uses; among candidates prefer prerequisites, then longer minutes, then id; stop when no step ends at s's start. The result, in start order, is the chain that fixes how early dinner must start. Example tests on H1 (work the expected chain out by hand in the design first) and on a schedule with a gap; a property: the chain is contiguous (each step starts when the previous ends), ends at the serving minute, and every step in it has slack 0 (P-UI id of your choice). In the UI: the `▲` mark and the words "On the critical path" go only to those steps (timeline and plan list); other steps show "Can slip N min" when slack > 0 and nothing when slack is 0. Remove "No slack" and the old "Critical" wording. `critical` in the schedule data stays as it is (G8 is untouched).

B. On a phone the header and the 2x2 flow navigation take the first third of the screen. Make the flow nav one compact row of four numbered steps ("1 Menu", "2 Steps", "3 Plan", "4 Cook"), each still a 48 px target, the current one marked by weight and an underline (not colour alone), unreachable ones dashed with their reason available as visible helper text only when tapped or focused (and always to screen readers). Put the theme switch and "Start over" behind one "Settings" disclosure button in the header on phones (inline on wide screens).

C. The timeline's time axis draws labels on top of each other near the serving line ("8:45 PM" under "Serve 9:00 PM"). Drop any tick label that would overlap the next label or the serve label (pure function in timeline-layout.ts, with a test), and let the serve label win.

D. public/assets/main.js is about 525 kB. Minify the production build in scripts/build-web.ts (keep watch mode unminified), report the before and after sizes, and keep the secret-marker guard working.

    - **Dish**: a named group of steps that is served. Every step belongs to exactly one dish.
    - **Terminal step**: a step that no other step lists in `after`; the last step of a dish.
    - **Hold** (`holdMin`): minutes a step's result can wait before the step that uses it starts
      (for a terminal step: before serving). `null` means the result **keeps**.
    - **Wait**: the minutes a result actually waits. A wait longer than its hold is a warning.
    - **Slack**: minutes a step can start later, everything else fixed, without breaking
      capacity, precedence or completeness.
    - **Critical**: in the schedule data, a step with slack 0. On screen the mark and the word
      "Critical" are reserved for slack 0 on a step whose result does not keep; a step whose result
      keeps and has slack 0 reads "No slack" (prep steps are packed together and could move as a
      block; decided in the plan-ui review, 2026-11-11).
    - **Lane**: one numbered unit of a resource (Burner 2, Oven 1, Cook 1). Timeline rows are lanes.
    - **Kitchen**: counts of burners, ovens and cooks.
    - **Serving time**: minute 0 on the scheduler's axis; earlier minutes are negative.
    - **Schedule**: a plan with a start minute and lanes for every step, plus slack and warnings.
    - **Rebuild**: recomputing the schedule during cooking after a slip, pinning done and
      running steps, with a diff of what moved.
    - **Pinned**: a done or running step whose times a rebuild may not change.
    - **Dinnerfile**: the Makefile export of a plan, readable back without the model.
    - **Issue**: a sanity-rule finding, either an error or a warning.

    describe('findForbidden (tech.md, The API key: the build fails if a key marker is in the bundle)', () => {
      it('finds nothing in a clean bundle', () => {

        writeFileSync(join(dir, 'main.js'), 'const k="sk-ant-api03-abc";');
        expect(findForbidden(dir)).toEqual([{ file: join(dir, 'main.js'), needle: 'sk-ant-' }]);
      });
    
      it('finds "ANTHROPIC" in a nested file, for example a source map', () => {
        mkdirSync(join(dir, 'maps'));
        writeFileSync(join(dir, 'maps', 'main.js.map'), '{"names":["process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"]}');

      });

      const usage = (r: Resource, minute: number): number => counts[r].get(minute) ?? 0;
      const isFull = (rs: readonly Resource[], minute: number): boolean =>
        rs.some((r) => usage(r, minute) + 1 > capacity[r]);
      const change = (rs: readonly Resource[], startMin: number, endMin: number, by: 1 | -1): void => {
        for (const r of rs) {
          for (let minute = startMin; minute < endMin; minute++) {
            counts[r].set(minute, usage(r, minute) + by);
          }
        }
      };
      return {
        usage,
        add: (rs, startMin, endMin) => change(rs, startMin, endMin, 1),
        remove: (rs, startMin, endMin) => change(rs, startMin, endMin, -1),
        lastFull(rs, startMin, endMin) {
          for (let minute = endMin - 1; minute >= startMin; minute--) {
            if (isFull(rs, minute)) return minute;
          }
          return null;
        },
        firstFull(rs, startMin, endMin) {
          for (let minute = startMin; minute < endMin; minute++) {

  it('CP8 a shared lane binds only when the resource is full (counterexample, fast-check seed -1350807787)', () => {
    // Two ovens. p [-3,-2) and q [-2,-1) both sit on oven 1, but at minute -2 only q is in an
    // oven (1 of 2), so delaying p by a minute breaks nothing: slack of p is 1, p is not on
    // the path. r [-1,0) waits for q. Path: q, r. (The first draft of the rule took p too.)
    const step = (id: string, after: string[]): Plan['steps'][number] => ({
      id,
      dish: 'x',
      title: id,
      minutes: 1,
      appliance: 'oven',
      handsOn: false,
      after,
      holdMin: id === 'x.p' ? null : 0,
    });
    const row = (id: string, startMin: number, slackMin: number): Schedule['steps'][number] => ({
      id,
      startMin,
      endMin: startMin + 1,
      lanes: { burner: null, oven: 1, cook: null },
      slackMin,
      critical: slackMin === 0,
      waitMin: id === 'x.p' ? null : 0,
    });
    const twoOvens: Schedule = {
      plan: {
        title: 'Two ovens',
        dishes: [{ id: 'x', name: 'X' }],
        steps: [step('x.p', []), step('x.q', []), step('x.r', ['x.q'])],
      },
      kitchen: { burners: 0, ovens: 2, cooks: 1 },
      steps: [row('x.p', -3, 1), row('x.q', -2, 0), row('x.r', -1, 0)],
      spanMin: 3,
      chainMin: 2,
      warnings: [],
    };
    expect(criticalPath(twoOvens)).toEqual(['x.q', 'x.r']);
  });
});
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

Fails on the assertion as expected. Now the fix: a lane binds only when its resource is full at the step's start.

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/core/critical-path.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("export type CriticalPathInput = Pick<Schedule, 'plan' | 'steps'> & { servingMin?: number };",
"export type CriticalPathInput = Pick<Schedule, 'plan' | 'steps' | 'kitchen'> & {\n  servingMin?: number;\n};")
old=s[s.index("function sharesLane"):s.index("/**\n * The critical path")]
new='''const RESOURCES = ['burner', 'oven', 'cook'] as const;

/**
 * Whether `b` holds a lane that `s` uses on a resource that is full at the minute `s` starts.
 * Only then does delaying `b` by a minute break capacity; with a free unit `b` is not binding.
 */
function bindsByLane(
  b: ScheduledStep,
  s: ScheduledStep,
  steps: readonly ScheduledStep[],
  kitchen: CriticalPathInput['kitchen'],
): boolean {
  const capacity = { burner: kitchen.burners, oven: kitchen.ovens, cook: kitchen.cooks };
  return RESOURCES.some((r) => {
    const lane = s.lanes[r];
    if (lane === null || b.lanes[r] !== lane) return false;
    const active = steps.filter(
      (x) => x.lanes[r] !== null && x.startMin <= s.startMin && s.startMin < x.endMin,
    ).length;
    return active >= capacity[r];
  });
}

'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace("(prerequisites.has(s.id) || sharesLane(s, here))","(prerequisites.has(s.id) || bindsByLane(s, here, schedule.steps, schedule.kitchen))")
s=s.replace("starts and is a prerequisite of `s` or holds a lane `s` uses; prerequisites first","starts and is a prerequisite of `s` or holds a lane `s` uses on a resource that is full at\n * that minute (otherwise delaying it would break nothing); prerequisites first")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

d='.kiro/specs/plan-ui/design.md'
s=open(d).read()

s=s.replace("`scheduleView`, `stepMark` (decided here) |","`scheduleView` (decided here) |")
s=s.replace("| `src/web/components/timeline-layout.ts`, `plan-list-items.ts` |","| `src/core/critical-path.ts` | `criticalPath(schedule)`: added in review, see \"The critical path\" |\n| `src/web/components/timeline-layout.ts`, `plan-list-items.ts` |")

# schedule view
a=s.index("export type StepMark")
b=s.index("```",a)
s=s[:a]+"```\n"[:0]+s[b:] if False else s
old=s[s.index("export type StepMark"):s.index("## Timeline layout")]
new='''```

`scheduleView` calls core `criticalPath` once and keeps the ids in `criticalPath`; nothing else in
`src/web` decides what is critical.

## The critical path (`src/core/critical-path.ts`, added in review, 2026-11-11)

"Slack 0" is not a useful mark: a just-in-time schedule gives every hot step slack 0 (14 of 17
steps of the Sunday roast). The word and the mark go to the chain that fixes how early dinner must
start.

```ts
export type CriticalPathInput = Pick<Schedule, 'plan' | 'steps' | 'kitchen'> & { servingMin?: number };
export function criticalPath(schedule: CriticalPathInput): string[]; // ids in start order; [] if nothing ends at servingMin
```

- Start: among the steps with `endMin === servingMin` (`servingMin` is 0 when absent) the one with
  the longest `minutes`, then the smallest id (`compareIds`).
- Binder of a step `s`: a step `b` with `b.endMin === s.startMin` that is a prerequisite of `s`
  (`s.after`), or holds a lane `s` uses (same resource, same number) on a resource that is full at
  minute `s.startMin` (as many steps in that resource then as the kitchen has units). Among
  candidates: prerequisites first, then the longer `minutes`, then the smaller id. The walk stops
  when `s` has no binder; the result is reversed into start order.
- Why "full": delaying `b` by a minute then puts one more step into a full resource, so `b` has
  slack 0. Without it a lane shared by luck (two ovens, one used) was taken as binding; the first
  draft did that and P-UI7 found it (seed -1350807787, example test CP8).
- Hand-worked on H1: carve `[-5,0)` is the only step ending at 0. Ending at -5: `chicken.rest` (a
  prerequisite) and `greens.toss` (holds cook 1, which carve uses); the prerequisite wins. rest
  starts at -25: `chicken.roast` ends there; roast starts at -100: `chicken.prep` ends there; prep
  starts at -110: nothing ends there. Chain: `chicken.prep`, `chicken.roast`, `chicken.rest`,
  `chicken.carve`, 110 minutes, the span.
- With a gap (prep moved to `[-112,-102)`): roast starts at -100, nothing ends there; chain: roast,
  rest, carve. `TWO_CHOPS` with one cook: `b.chop [-20,-10)` binds `a.chop [-10,0)` through
  cook 1. `HANDS_FREE` with `BIG_KITCHEN`: `jelly.set` (120) over `stock.simmer` (60).
- P-UI7 (`test/core/critical-path.pbt.ts`): over `arbPlanAndKitchen()` with `schedule`, the chain is
  contiguous (each start is the previous end), ends at serving, has no repeats, every step in it has
  slack 0, and it is empty exactly when no step ends at serving.
- On screen: `▲` and "On the critical path" for these steps only (timeline bars and plan list).
  Any other step shows `Can slip <duration>` when its slack is above 0 and nothing when it is 0.
  `critical` in the schedule data stays slack 0 (G8).

'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace("`critical = stepMark === 'critical'`.","`critical = view.criticalPath.has(id)`.")
s=s.replace("critical: boolean; // ▲ drawn only for 'critical'","critical: boolean; // ▲ drawn only for steps on the critical path")
s=s.replace("""`chicken.prep` is slack 0 and keeps: "No slack", no `▲`.""","""Only `chicken.prep`, `chicken.roast`, `chicken.rest` and `chicken.carve` are on the critical path
(hand-worked above), so only their bars carry `▲`; the roux, simmer, boil, strain and toss bars
have slack 0 and none.""")
s=s.replace("export type ItemStatus = { kind: 'critical' } | { kind: 'back-to-back' } | { kind: 'slack'; minutes: number };","export type ItemStatus = { kind: 'critical' } | { kind: 'slack'; minutes: number } | { kind: 'none' };")
# list table
a=s.index("| heading | range | title | dish | lanes | duration | status |")
b=s.index("H3 (`TIGHT_OVEN`)")
tbl=s[a:b]
tbl=tbl.replace("| No slack |","| ▲ On the critical path |").replace("Slack 1 h 15 min","Can slip 1 h 15 min").replace("▲ Critical","▲ On the critical path")
for t in ['Make a roux in the roasting tin','Add stock and let the gravy thicken','Cook the greens in salted water','Strain the gravy into a jug','Toss the greens with butter']:
    i=tbl.index(t); j=tbl.index("▲ On the critical path",i)
    tbl=tbl[:j]+""+tbl[j+len("▲ On the critical path"):]
s=s[:a]+tbl.rstrip()+"\n\nThe status cell is empty for a step with slack 0 off the critical path.\n\n"+s[b:]
s=s.replace("| list | `▲ Critical`, `No slack`, `Slack <duration>`, final item `Serve` |","| list | `▲ On the critical path`, `Can slip <duration>` (nothing at slack 0), final item `Serve` |")
s=s.replace("| `test/web/components/plan-list-items.test.ts` (+ `.pbt.ts`) | H1 and H3 tables,","| `test/core/critical-path.test.ts` (+ `.pbt.ts`) | the chains above, tie-breaks, a gap, a rebuilt-shaped input, CP8; P-UI7 |\n| `test/web/components/plan-list-items.test.ts` (+ `.pbt.ts`) | H1 and H3 tables,")
open(d,'w').write(s)

/**

cat > src/web/components/Header.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useId, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { hashOf } from '../state/route.js';
import { StartOver } from './StartOver.js';
import { ThemeSwitch } from './ThemeSwitch.js';

/**
 * On every screen: the brand link to the Menu, the theme switch and "Start over" (R9.1). On a
 * phone the last two sit behind one "Settings" disclosure button; from 40rem up they are inline
 * and the button is not shown (plan-ui review B).
 */
export function Header(): VNode {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const panelId = useId();
  return (
    <header class="app-header no-print">
      <a class="brand" href={hashOf('menu')}>
        {COPY.brand}
      </a>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="button settings-toggle"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls={panelId}
        onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
      >
        {COPY.settings}
      </button>
      <div
        id={panelId}
        class={open ? 'app-header__tools app-header__tools--open' : 'app-header__tools'}
      >
        <ThemeSwitch />
        <StartOver />
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/FlowNav.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useId, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { hashOf } from '../state/route.js';
import { reachable } from '../state/store.js';
import type { Route } from '../state/store.js';

const ITEMS: ReadonlyArray<{ route: Route; label: string }> = [
  { route: 'menu', label: COPY.navMenu },
  { route: 'steps', label: COPY.navSteps },
  { route: 'plan', label: COPY.navPlan },
  { route: 'cook', label: COPY.navCook },
];

/**
 * Menu, Steps, Plan, Cook as one compact row of numbered steps (R9.2, plan-ui review B).
 * An unreachable step is a dashed, focusable button with `aria-disabled`; its reason is always
 * its accessible description and shows as visible text only while it is tapped or focused.
 */
export function FlowNav(props: { cookRegistered: boolean }): VNode {
  const route = useAppState((state) => state.route);
  const reach = useAppState((state) => reachable(state, props.cookRegistered));
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<Route | null>(null);
  const baseId = useId();
  const shownState = shown === null ? null : reach[shown];
  return (
    <nav class="flow-nav no-print" aria-label={COPY.navLabel}>
      <ol>
        {ITEMS.map((item, index) => {
          const state = reach[item.route];
          const number = (
            <span class="flow-nav__number" aria-hidden="true">
              {index + 1}{' '}
            </span>
          );
          if (!state.ok) {
            const reasonId = `${baseId}-${item.route}`;
            return (
              <li key={item.route} class="flow-nav__item flow-nav__item--off">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  class="flow-nav__off"
                  aria-disabled="true"
                  aria-describedby={reasonId}
                  onClick={() => setShown(item.route)}
                  onFocus={() => setShown(item.route)}
                  onBlur={() => setShown((current) => (current === item.route ? null : current))}
                >
                  {number}
                  {item.label}
                </button>
                <span id={reasonId} class="visually-hidden">
                  {state.reason}
                </span>
              </li>
            );
          }
          return (
            <li key={item.route} class="flow-nav__item">
              <a
                href={hashOf(item.route)}
                aria-current={item.route === route ? 'step' : undefined}
                onClick={() => setShown(null)}
              >
                {number}
                {item.label}
              </a>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
      <p class="flow-nav__helper muted" aria-hidden="true">
        {shownState !== null && !shownState.ok ? shownState.reason : ''}
      </p>
    </nav>
  );
}
EOF

s=s[:a]+'''/* Flow navigation: one compact row of four numbered steps */
.flow-nav ol {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: var(--space-2);
  margin: 0;
  padding: var(--space-2) 0;
  list-style: none;
}

.flow-nav__item {
  display: flex;

p='src/web/components/timeline-layout.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("export function layoutTimeline(","""/** Width of one monospace character at the 18 px base size (0.6 em), for tick label spans. */
export const TICK_CHAR_PX = 10.8;
/** A tick's left rule and padding, added to its label. */
export const TICK_PAD_PX = 5;

/**
 * Drops tick labels that would overlap (review C). The serve label is right-aligned at
 * `serveLeftPx` and wins: a tick at or after the serve line, or whose label would reach the
 * serve label, goes. Among the rest, left to right, a tick that would start before the
 * previous kept label ends goes. Widths are estimated from the character count, because this
 * module has no DOM.
 */
export function thinTicks(
  ticks: readonly TimelineTick[],
  serveLeftPx: number,
  serveLabelText: string,
): TimelineTick[] {
  const serveStart = serveLeftPx - serveLabelText.length * TICK_CHAR_PX;
  const kept: TimelineTick[] = [];
  let previousEnd = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
  for (const tick of ticks) {
    if (tick.leftPx >= serveLeftPx) continue;
    const end = tick.leftPx + tick.label.length * TICK_CHAR_PX + TICK_PAD_PX;
    if (end > serveStart || tick.leftPx < previousEnd) continue;
    kept.push(tick);
    previousEnd = end;
  }
  return kept;
}

export function layoutTimeline(""",1)
s=s.replace("""    ticks,
    serveLeftPx: x(view.servingMin),
    serveLabel: serveLabel(formatClock(clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin), locale)),
  };""","""    ticks: thinTicks(ticks, serveLeftPx, serveText),
    serveLeftPx,
    serveLabel: serveText,
  };""")
s=s.replace("  return {\n    originMin,","  const serveLeftPx = x(view.servingMin);\n  const serveText = serveLabel(formatClock(clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin), locale));\n  return {\n    originMin,",1)

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/web/copy.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import * as copy from '../../src/web/copy.js';

const original = process.env['TZ'];
beforeAll(() => {
  process.env['TZ'] = 'Europe/London';
  expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
});
afterAll(() => {
  if (original === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
  else process.env['TZ'] = original;
});

const AT_1702 = Date.parse('2026-11-11T17:02:00Z');

/** Every constant string of copy.ts, by name. */
function constants(): [string, string][] {
  const tables = {
    COPY: copy.COPY,
    EDIT_ERRORS: copy.EDIT_ERRORS,
    STEPS_COPY: copy.STEPS_COPY,
    EDITOR_COPY: copy.EDITOR_COPY,
    MANAGE_COPY: copy.MANAGE_COPY,
    PASTE_COPY: copy.PASTE_COPY,
    DINNERFILE_COPY: copy.DINNERFILE_COPY,
    SHARE_COPY: copy.SHARE_COPY,
    EXPORT_COPY: copy.EXPORT_COPY,
  };
  return Object.entries(tables).flatMap(([table, entries]) =>
    Object.entries(entries).map(([key, value]): [string, string] => [`${table}.${key}`, value]),
  );
}

/** Every copy function on fixed arguments, singular and plural, by call. */
function results(): [string, string][] {
  const failures = [
    { kind: 'bad_request' },
    { kind: 'input_too_long' },
    { kind: 'model_unavailable' },
    { kind: 'model_refused' },
    { kind: 'model_output_invalid' },
    { kind: 'internal' },
    { kind: 'network' },
    { kind: 'rate_limited', retryAtMs: AT_1702 },
    { kind: 'rate_limited', retryAtMs: null },
    { kind: 'daily_cap_reached', retryAtMs: AT_1702 },
    { kind: 'daily_cap_reached', retryAtMs: null },
    { kind: 'unexpected', status: 502 },
  ] as const;
  const calls: [string, string][] = [
    ['documentTitle', copy.documentTitle('Plan')],
    ['dishesLine', copy.dishesLine(['Roast chicken', 'Gravy'])],
    ['replaceAsk', copy.replaceAsk('Sunday roast')],
    ['listWait', copy.listWait(40, 10)],
    ['slackLabel', copy.slackLabel(75)],
    ['laneName', copy.laneName('oven', 1)],
    ['fewer', copy.fewer('Burners')],
    ['more', copy.more('Burners')],
    ['startAt', copy.startAt('17:40')],
    ['serveAt', copy.serveAt('00:30', 'tomorrow')],
    ['takes', copy.takes('1 h 50 min')],
    ['holdWarning terminal', copy.holdWarning('Pie', 'Bake the pie', 40, 10, null)],
    ['holdWarning user', copy.holdWarning('Pie', 'Bake the pie', 40, 10, 'Serve the pie')],
    ['missingAppliance oven', copy.missingAppliance('oven', ['Roast the chicken'])],
    ['missingAppliance burner', copy.missingAppliance('burner', ['Boil the greens', 'Make gravy'])],
    ['pastStart', copy.pastStart('1 h 50 min', '19:35')],
    ['serveAtButton', copy.serveAtButton('19:35')],
    ['serveLabel', copy.serveLabel('19:30')],
    ['removedStep 0', copy.removedStep('Roast the chicken', [])],
    ['removedStep 2', copy.removedStep('Roast the chicken', ['Rest', 'Make roux'])],
    ['removedDish 0', copy.removedDish('Gravy', [])],
    ['stepsIntro', copy.stepsIntro('Sunday roast')],
    ['issueSummary 1 1', copy.issueSummary(1, 1)],
    ['issueSummary 2 2', copy.issueSummary(2, 2)],
    ['makeBlocked 1', copy.makeBlocked(1)],
    ['makeBlocked 2', copy.makeBlocked(2)],
    ['applianceLabel', copy.applianceLabel('oven')],
    ['handsLabel', copy.handsLabel(true)],
    ['holdLabel keeps', copy.holdLabel(null)],
    ['holdLabel 5', copy.holdLabel(5)],
    ['waitsFor 0', copy.waitsFor([])],
    ['waitsFor 2', copy.waitsFor(['Prep', 'Roast'])],
    ['removeStepAsk 0', copy.removeStepAsk('Roast the chicken', [])],
    ['removeStepAsk 1', copy.removeStepAsk('Roast the chicken', ['Rest'])],
    ['removeStepAsk 2', copy.removeStepAsk('Roast the chicken', ['Rest', 'Make roux'])],
    ['removeDishAsk 1', copy.removeDishAsk('Gravy', 1, [])],
    ['removeDishAsk 3', copy.removeDishAsk('Gravy', 3, ['Carve'])],
    ['recipeCounter', copy.recipeCounter(12000)],
    ['elapsedSeconds', copy.elapsedSeconds(5)],
    ['showLine', copy.showLine(57)],
    ['shareTooLong', copy.shareTooLong(9000)],
  ];
  for (const failure of failures) {
    calls.push([`parseFailureText ${failure.kind}`, copy.parseFailureText(failure, 'en-GB')]);
  }
  return calls;
}

const all = (): [string, string][] => [...constants(), ...results()];

describe('copy: ui.md "Copy" rules (R10.3, N5, N6)', () => {
  it('N5 the copy has strings to check', () => {
    expect(constants().length).toBeGreaterThan(100);
    expect(results().length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
  });

  it('R10.3 no exclamation mark except the warning mark that opens a plan list warning', () => {
    const bad = all().filter(([, text]) => text.replace(/^! Warning: /, '').includes('!'));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('R10.3 the warning mark is followed by "Warning"', () => {
    for (const [name, text] of all()) {
      if (text.startsWith('!')) expect([name, text.startsWith('! Warning: ')]).toEqual([name, true]);
    }
  });

  it('N6 no "task" or "job" (the glossary says step)', () => {
    const bad = all().filter(([, text]) => /\b(tasks?|jobs?)\b/i.test(text));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('R10.3 no emoji; the marks ▲ ✓ and the dashes are not emoji', () => {
    const bad = all().filter(([, text]) =>
      /\p{Extended_Pictographic}/u.test(text.replace(/[▲✓]/g, '')),
    );
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('N5 no ANTHROPIC and no key marker', () => {
    const bad = all().filter(([, text]) => /anthropic|sk-ant-/i.test(text));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('R10.3 labels, headings and buttons are in sentence case: only the first word and proper names are capitalised', () => {
    const PROPER = new Set(['Dinnerfile', 'Make', 'Dinner', 'GNU']);
    // A label is a short string without sentence punctuation.
    const labels = constants().filter(([, text]) => text.split(' ').length <= 6 && !/[.?:!]/.test(text));
    expect(labels.length).toBeGreaterThan(60);
    const bad = labels.filter(([, text]) =>
      text
        .split(' ')
        .slice(1)
        .some((word) => /^[A-Z]/.test(word) && !PROPER.has(word)),
    );
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('R10.3 sentences start with a capital letter or a bracket and end with a full stop, question mark or colon', () => {
    const sentences = all().filter(([, text]) => text.split(' ').length > 6);
    const bad = sentences.filter(
      ([, text]) => !/^[A-Z(\u201c!▲]/.test(text) || !/[.?:]$|\bmin$/.test(text),
    );
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('copy: plurals (1 and 2)', () => {
  it('R4.10 error and warning counts read 1 error and 1 warning, 2 errors and 2 warnings', () => {
    expect(copy.issueSummary(1, 1)).toBe('1 error and 1 warning.');
    expect(copy.issueSummary(2, 2)).toBe('2 errors and 2 warnings.');
    expect(copy.issueSummary(0, 0)).toBe('0 errors and 0 warnings.');
  });
  it('R4.10 the blocked reason reads Fix 1 error, Fix 2 errors', () => {
    expect(copy.makeBlocked(1)).toBe('Fix 1 error to make the plan.');
    expect(copy.makeBlocked(2)).toBe('Fix 2 errors to make the plan.');
  });
  it('R4.7 removing a step names one dependent in the singular and two in the plural', () => {
    expect(copy.removeStepAsk('Roast', ['Rest'])).toBe(
      'Remove \u201cRoast\u201d? 1 step waits for it: Rest. It will no longer wait for it.',
    );
    expect(copy.removeStepAsk('Roast', ['Rest', 'Roux'])).toBe(
      'Remove \u201cRoast\u201d? 2 steps wait for it: Rest, Roux. They will no longer wait for it.',
    );
  });
  it('R4.7 removing a dish counts its steps: 1 step, 3 steps', () => {
    expect(copy.removeDishAsk('Gravy', 1, [])).toBe('Remove the dish Gravy and its 1 step?');
    expect(copy.removeDishAsk('Gravy', 3, [])).toBe('Remove the dish Gravy and its 3 steps?');
  });
  it('R4.7 the removal notice says "no longer wait" for the steps that waited', () => {
    expect(copy.removedStep('Roast', ['Rest'])).toBe(
      'Removed \u201cRoast\u201d. Rest no longer wait for it.',
    );
  });
});
EOF

Now the a11y spec (T8.2 and T8.3 together in one file, as the tasks say).

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > e2e/a11y.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Plan } from '../src/core/index.js';
import {
  HOME_KITCHEN,
  SUNDAY_ROAST,
  SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE,
} from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import {
  expectNoAxeViolations,
  expectNoPageScroll,
  FIXED_NOW,
  fakeParse,
  parseError,
  seedDinner,
} from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
});

/** SUNDAY_ROAST with `gravy.roux` waiting for a step that does not exist (an error). */
const BROKEN_ROAST: Plan = {
  ...SUNDAY_ROAST,
  steps: SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.map((step) =>
    step.id === 'gravy.roux' ? { ...step, after: ['chicken.rost'] } : step,
  ),
};

/** Every visible button, input, select and text area is at least 48×48 px (ui.md, N1). */
async function expectTargets48(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const small = await page
    .locator('button, input:not([type="hidden"]), select, textarea')
    .evaluateAll((elements) =>
      elements.flatMap((el) => {
        const box = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const style = getComputedStyle(el);
        if (box.width === 0 || box.height === 0 || style.visibility === 'hidden') return [];
        // The file picker and the visually hidden helpers are not targets.
        if (el.closest('.visually-hidden') !== null) return [];
        if (box.width >= 48 && box.height >= 48) return [];
        const name = el.getAttribute('aria-label') ?? el.textContent ?? el.getAttribute('id') ?? '';
        return [`${el.tagName.toLowerCase()} "${name.trim().slice(0, 40)}" ${Math.round(box.width)}x${Math.round(box.height)}`];
      }),
    );
  expect(small).toEqual([]);
}

async function check(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectTargets48(page);
}

for (const scheme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
  test.describe(`a11y, ${scheme} scheme`, () => {
    test.use({ colorScheme: scheme });

    test('R11.3 Menu', async ({ page }) => {
      await page.goto('/');
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 3 }).first()).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Menu with a parse error', async ({ page }) => {
      await fakeParse(page, 429, parseError('rate_limited'), { 'Retry-After': '120' });
      await page.goto('/');
      await page.getByLabel('Recipe text').fill('Roast the chicken for 75 minutes.');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Read recipes' }).click();
      await expect(page.getByText(/^You have read several recipes/)).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Menu with Dinnerfile errors', async ({ page }) => {
      await page.goto('/');
      await page.getByLabel('Dinnerfile text').fill(`${SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE}$(X)\n`);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open Dinnerfile' }).click();
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Show line 57' })).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Steps with an editor open and an error', async ({ page }) => {
      await seedDinner(page, BROKEN_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30', 'steps');
      await page.goto('/');
      await page
        .locator('[data-step-id="gravy.roux"]')
        .getByRole('button', { name: 'Edit' })
        .click();
      await expect(page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Waits for' })).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Plan', async ({ page }) => {
      await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
      await page.goto('/');
      await expect(page.getByText('Start at 17:40')).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Plan with the Dinnerfile panel open', async ({ page }) => {
      await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
      await page.goto('/');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Dinnerfile', exact: true }).click();
      await expect(page.locator('pre').first()).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Plan with the share panel open', async ({ page }) => {
      await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
      await page.goto('/');
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share link' }).click();
      await expect(page.getByLabel('Link to this dinner')).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });

    test('R11.3 Plan with the settings open (phone) and a missing appliance', async ({ page }) => {
      await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, { ...HOME_KITCHEN, ovens: 0 }, '19:30');
      await page.goto('/');
      await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add an oven' })).toBeVisible();
      await check(page);
    });
  });
}

test.describe('zoom and motion', () => {
  const SCREENS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, (page: Page) => Promise<void>]> = [
    ['Menu', async (page) => void (await page.goto('/'))],
    [
      'Steps',
      async (page) => {
        await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30', 'steps');
        await page.goto('/');
      },
    ],
    [
      'Plan',
      async (page) => {
        await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
        await page.goto('/');
      },
    ],
  ];

  for (const [name, open] of SCREENS) {
    test(`R6.9 N1 ${name} at 195 px wide (390 px at 200%) does not scroll the page sideways`, async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      await page.setViewportSize({ width: 195, height: 844 });
      await open(page);
      await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
      await expectNoPageScroll(page);
    });
  }

  test('N1 under reduced motion no element has a running transition or animation', async ({
    browser,
  }) => {
    const context = await browser.newContext({
      reducedMotion: 'reduce',
      locale: 'en-GB',
      timezoneId: 'Europe/London',
    });
    const page = await context.newPage();
    await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(FIXED_NOW) });
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Dinnerfile', exact: true }).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Fewer burners' }).click();
    const running = await page.evaluate(() => document.getAnimations().length);
    expect(running).toBe(0);
    const durations = await page
      .locator('body *')
      .evaluateAll((els) =>
        els.filter((el) => {
          const s = getComputedStyle(el);
          return s.transitionDuration.split(',').some((d) => parseFloat(d) > 0);
        }).length,
      );
    expect(durations).toBe(0);
    await context.close();
  });
});
EOF

    /** A count with "−" and "+" buttons, disabled at the bounds (R5.1). */
    export function Stepper(props: Props): VNode {
      const id = `stepper-${props.label.toLowerCase()}`;
      return (
        <div class="stepper" role="group" aria-labelledby={id}>
          <span id={id} class="stepper__label">
            {props.label}
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button stepper__button"
            aria-label={fewer(props.label)}
            disabled={props.value <= props.min}
            onClick={() => props.onChange(props.value - 1)}
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">−</span>
          </button>
          <output class="stepper__value">{props.value}</output>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button stepper__button"
            aria-label={more(props.label)}
            disabled={props.value >= props.max}
            onClick={() => props.onChange(props.value + 1)}
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">+</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }

cat > e2e/perf.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import { seedDinner } from './support.js';

// N2: on a phone the largest sample's timeline shows within 1 s of the tap, and a stepper tap
// updates the list within 200 ms. Times are taken in the page with performance.now(), from
// the click to the first frame in which the condition holds. These run on the real clock.
test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

test.beforeEach(({}, info) => {
  expect(info.project.name).toBe('phone');
});

interface SampleSummary {
  title: string;
  plan: { steps: unknown[] };
}

/** Clicks the element in the page and waits for `condition`; resolves with the elapsed ms. */
async function timedClick(
  page: Page,
  selector: string,
  index: number,
  condition: 'timeline' | 'list-changed',
): Promise<number> {
  return page.evaluate(
    ({ selector, index, condition }) =>
      new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
        const target = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(selector)[index];
        if (target === undefined) {
          reject(new Error(`no element ${selector}[${index}]`));
          return;
        }
        const before = document.getElementById('plan-list')?.innerText ?? '';
        const started = performance.now();
        target.click();
        const poll = (): void => {
          const done =
            condition === 'timeline'
              ? (() => {
                  const box = document.querySelector('.timeline .bar')?.getBoundingClientRect();
                  return box !== undefined && box.width > 0 && box.height > 0;
                })()
              : (document.getElementById('plan-list')?.innerText ?? '') !== before;
          if (done) resolve(performance.now() - started);
          else if (performance.now() - started > 5000) reject(new Error('condition not met in 5 s'));
          else requestAnimationFrame(poll);
        };
        requestAnimationFrame(poll);
      }),
    { selector, index, condition },
  );
}

test.describe('speed (N2)', () => {
  test('N2 the largest sample shows its timeline within 1 s of the tap', async ({ page }) => {
    const response = await page.request.get('/api/samples');
    const body = (await response.json()) as { samples: SampleSummary[] };
    const sizes = body.samples.map((s) => s.plan.steps.length);
    const largest = sizes.indexOf(Math.max(...sizes));
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Plan this dinner' })).toHaveCount(
      body.samples.length,
    );
    const elapsed = await timedClick(page, 'button.button--primary', largest, 'timeline');
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
      body.samples[largest]?.title ?? '',
    );
    expect(elapsed).toBeLessThan(1000);
  });

  test('N2 a stepper tap updates the plan list within 200 ms', async ({ page }) => {
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, { ...HOME_KITCHEN, burners: 2 }, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page.locator('#plan-list')).toContainText('Make a roux in the roasting tin');
    const elapsed = await timedClick(
      page,
      'button[aria-label="Fewer burners"]',
      0,
      'list-changed',
    );
    expect(elapsed).toBeLessThan(200);
  });
});
EOF

T8 is done and plan-ui is complete. The four review changes (A–D) are in, and every sub-task T8.1–T8.5 is ticked after its command passed in this session. Nothing is committed and no dev server was started.

## What changed

**A. Critical path.**
- New `criticalPath(schedule)` in `src/core/critical-path.ts`, exported from `index.ts`, working for `Schedule` and `RebuiltSchedule`.
- It starts from the step that ends at serving, taking the longest `minutes`, then the smallest id.
- It walks back through binders, preferring prerequisites, then longer `minutes`, then id.
- On the Sunday roast it marks 4 steps instead of 14: prep, roast, rest, carve (worked out by hand in the design).
- The UI shows `▲ On the critical path` only for those steps, "Can slip N min" when slack is above 0, and nothing at slack 0. "No slack" and the old "Critical" wording are gone, and `critical` in the schedule data is unchanged.
- **Deviation from your rule:** a step that only shares a lane binds the path only when that resource is full at the step's start. Your plain rule failed P-UI7 (seed -1350807787), because a lane shared by luck (two ovens, one used) gives slack 13. It is now the named test CP8. I updated the requirements, the design ("The critical path"), `product.md` "Critical" and the `ui.md` wording to match.

**B. Navigation and header.**
- The flow nav is one row of "1 Menu … 4 Cook", with the current step marked by weight and underline.
- Unreachable steps are dashed buttons with `aria-disabled`. Their reason is always their accessible description and shows as text only when tapped or focused.
- Theme and "Start over" sit behind a "Settings" disclosure on phones and are inline from 40rem up.
- The phone header plus nav now take about 130 px instead of about 270.

**C. Timeline axis.** `thinTicks` drops a tick label that would overlap the next one or the serve label, and the serve label wins. The widths are estimated from character count (10.8 px per character) because the module has no DOM. One consequence: a serving time of 00:30 loses its "Thu 00:00" tick label; the plan list still carries the day.

**D. Bundle size.**
- **Premise was wrong.** The production build was already minified (`minify: !watch`); it was 571,239 bytes (~558 kB as esbuild reports it).
- **Real cause.** zod's locale index ships every language, about 240 kB.
- **Fix.** I added a small esbuild plugin that keeps English only.
- **Result.** `main.js` is now 310,075 bytes (302.8 kB). The secret-marker guard is unchanged, and `webBuildOptions` is exported with a test.

**T8.**
- `test/web/copy.test.ts` (T8.1).
- `e2e/a11y.spec.ts` (T8.2 and T8.3): axe, 48 px targets, 195 px width, reduced motion.
- `e2e/perf.spec.ts` (T8.4): timeline visible 40 ms after the tap on phone (46 ms on desktop), stepper update 9 ms on phone (3 ms on desktop).

**Fixes the new tests exposed.**
- Checkboxes were 27 px. They are now custom 48 px boxes with a ✓ mark.
- The nav and steppers overflowed at 195 px. They now wrap, and the stepper label gets its own row.
- Removal notice grammar: it said "Rest no longer wait", and now says "Rest no longer waits" for one step.

## Slips and not verified

- **Test order:** for the A plan-list tests, `thinTicks` and the minify plugin I wrote the tests before the code. For `thinTicks`, and for the A tests when I changed the code, I did not see the red run first. For the plugin I removed it and confirmed the size assertion fails.
